# Compare regressions from the fresh ASv6.100 fifty-note hierarchy

This is an additional **Compare development panel**, not a Bootstrap test or a
new hierarchy. The user authorized adding the failures identified in the
[fresh fifty-note findings](hierarchy-as100-fifty-note-findings-v1.md) to the
Compare regression set. The original construction used unchanged Bootstrap-v96
and Compare-v48. Compare-v69 and approved-for-qualification Compare-v75 completed
the initial experimental trials. Experimental V183 retains reversal conflicts,
and V184's focused ambiguity-instruction test also fails its consistency gate.
Neither is adopted; V179 remains the symmetric A/B development baseline. V176's
subsequent
[qualified-subset test](#qualified-subset-test-of-unchanged-v176) retains two
settled reversals and additional input qualifications. The
[input-validity audit](#input-validity-audit-before-further-compare-edits) separates
provisional accuracy candidates, ambiguous meanings and consistency fixtures
before further edits. Original results, producer receipts and actual user
decisions remain preserved below; review counts are not accuracy rates.

## Current Cookware / Stockpots qualification

The user reopened the interpretation after the V183 review: **"both state that
they are DESIGNED for these things"** and **"it really ammounts to whether stock
is considered food"**. The user then agreed that the demonstrated problem is
incompatible relationships for unchanged definitions, while which label is wrong
has not been established, and authorized correcting the evaluation records.
Neither `neither` nor Cookware containment is a current semantic target for this
exact pair. This qualification does not require the model to return `uncertain`;
it qualifies our evaluation expectation.

A read-only producer audit verifies the exact Bootstrap-v96 meanings and native
receipts used by `isolated-035-forward/reverse`:

- Cookware: original `verified-prefix-v3/step-007/meaning/experiment`, receipt
  `d3a81ab5c56f073e61b8dbc2319846fdb5183978e52737f31b964de0804b1e43`.
  Its resolution says: "Qualifying vessels are designed for cooking food; their
  intended cooking applications may include the Full English Breakfast or other
  meals." The named breakfast is explicitly illustrative. The producer does not
  separately settle whether the final phrase "for preparing meals" restricts
  every qualifying design or describes its culinary application.
- Stockpots: original `verified-prefix-v3/step-014/meaning/experiment`, receipt
  `9e5dc52d3dc93697fe4c80bdc712f35fd55c138d9ce164326e3476aa4e8d74cb`.
  It resolves stock-making design versus actual use for the illustrated batch;
  it does not separately settle the food/meal implication now disputed. Actual
  use or disposal alone cannot establish a different design purpose.

Both frozen producer templates are Bootstrap-v96, SHA-256
`599e811307cfd3feac290ec91b0c763f1900cc244fccc4756d8255027a050845`, with verified
ASv6.100 source provenance. Their final strings exactly match the Compare inputs.
Source evidence and producer diagnostics remain outside those model inputs and
cannot supply additional restrictions. No meaning or prompt is rewritten.

Earlier native accept/reject counts, forced `neither` targets and laboratory
exclusion claims below describe the historical review under the now-reopened
reading. They are not current semantic error counts or evidence of a reliable
purpose-inference fix. Original outputs, user decisions, agent reviews, receipts,
usage and frozen targets remain preserved. Current agent reviews qualify the
exact pair as unresolved without rewriting user-role review history. Reversal
conflicts and response changes remain observable independently of a gold label.

The native correction appends agent qualifications to 66 exact-pair responses
across 33 experiments and eight laboratory-case diagnostic responses across
four experiments. The original review history remains intact; four commercial
stockpot diagnostics are unchanged. Every affected experiment retains native
`scope-qualification-before.json` and `scope-qualified.json` snapshots. Frozen
inputs, producer receipts, generation bytes and consistency reports are unchanged:
the exact-pair outputs still contain 44 Cookware-containment labels, 22 `neither`
labels and 18 reversal conflicts across 33 paired draws. These overlapping
development draws are not independent semantic cases. Correction requires no
inference.

For the subsequent development panels, separate the four control pairs from
this one unresolved semantic pair:

V183's former 21/24 support count is historical; its eight Stockpots responses
are currently unresolved. All 16 tested controls still pass, and its three

original all-supported/no-conflict/repeat-stability gate still does not pass.

The completed [fresh eight-request matched diagnostic](#completed-fresh-matched-order-diagnostic)
reproduces Cookware containment when Cookware is physically first and `neither`

The [matched V179/V184 test](#completed-matched-v179v184-test) preserves all

## Frozen selection

hierarchy or the separate regenerated ASv6.100 panel. Original posts, selected
subjects, contexts and complete producer trees remain source-bound. The
source collection's native provenance identifies 50 ASv6.100 answers.

## What the panel checks

| Family | Required assessment |
| --- | --- |
| Eating/drinking | Preserve the incoming Food meaning's eating and nourishment boundaries across Food, Dinner and Coffee counterparts. |
| Food/preparation/appliances | Keep edible-material scope fixed while assessing preparation and its device descendants. |
| Food/cookware/stockpots | A vessel's food-preparation purpose does not establish that it is edible material. Assess the whole supplied branch. |
| Nourishment/intermediate Food/Coffee | An intermediate consumption-only definition cannot silently remove a required nourishment condition. |
| Food dishes/Bread/Bread preparation | Use one information-topic convention and preserve the meal-use restriction throughout the chain. |
| Meal-use accepted edges | Check Bread, Salsa, pie preparation, bagel making and sourdough preparation without assuming a meal role absent from their fixed scope. |
| Optional roles | Review Butter and Tomatoes as whole topics rather than selecting a convenient cooking role. |
| Conjunction/state | Preserve Salsa's ingredients being intended **and prepared**; distinguish the independent Breakfast concern. |
| Qualified nourishment cases | Leave Eating and the author's coffee open to explicit scope review. |
| Controls | Preserve supported particular-fatigue, sleepiness and explicit-dinner containment alongside the fatigue/Mental-states negative control. |

The Food/appliances, Food/stockpots, nourishment bridge and Bread/preparation
families each have all three pairwise links in both orientations. Additional
Food/Coffee comparisons close the eating/drinking counterpart triangle. Existing
canonical edges nominate these comparisons; they do not supply answers or gold.

Review metadata is outside model input. All expectations are provisional agent
assessments; none is a new human judgment. Historical Compare-v48 answers are
referenced as witnesses, not copied into inputs or treated as desired labels.
Snapshot expectations comprise six provisional containment controls, two
`neither` controls and two `uncertain` controls; the other 22 have no exact label
target. Missing meal-use cases carry provisional objections to positive
containment, while Butter, Eating and the author's coffee remain semantic-open.

No snapshot label, branch uncertainty or branch-only reason check is transferred
to an isolated comparison. Bread at original steps 059 and 067 remains two
different producer definitions, not an exact-input repeat.

## Evaluation limits

Native `show --json` checks the 70 isolated requests for reversals, transitivity,
equivalence substitution and cycles using exact producer identities. It explicitly
excludes the 32 original snapshots from that denominator. Complete isolated chains
are evaluated from their actual new pair answers, without assuming the historical
graph edges are correct. Failed, unrun or uncertain answers remain unresolved.

The original snapshots still require attributed semantic and reason review,
including whole-child and ancestor restrictions. A zero contradiction count does
not certify the eating/drinking boundary, the conjunction reading or any accepted
edge. Rejecting every relationship would lose the positive controls; returning
uncertain throughout would leave the panel unresolved. Contradiction witnesses
share inputs and history and are not independent accuracy observations.

This panel supplements the existing 196-request ASv6.100 qualification panel and
its branch fixtures. It does not replace them or modify their frozen cases.
Selecting these witnesses alone establishes neither failure nor repair by
Compare-v69; its actual new results are recorded below.

The source resolves native receipts and historical trials in the retained

```bash
cd /home/dev/src/notedeck_worktrees/algos/oracle-mvp-compare-disambiguation-v1
../experiment-runner/bin/experiment-runner --project-root . prepare \
```

trial is recorded below. No Bootstrap generation, prompt edit or hierarchy change
was performed.

one of the 32 original reconstructed `INPUT_JSON` strings and input hashes was
checked against its valid original native Compare record and matched exactly.
Before execution, the isolated scorer reported 70 eligible requests, 32 excluded

## First unchanged Compare-v69 trial

The shared runner completed **102 valid case outputs** using **101 actual
attempts**: 100 newly dispatched comparisons and one cache warmup, plus two exact
answer reuses within this new panel. There were no failed attempts, retries or
missing usage. Every response copied its supplied local meaning exactly.
The existing persistent 20,000-attempt counter was used without resetting it.
Native API-equivalent cost is **$0.3433892**, including warmup; subscription
incremental cash is zero. Reused outputs are not additional independent draws.

Native consistency checks retain **10 registered reversal witnesses**, representing
**nine distinct exact-input pairs**. Isolated pairs 008 and 033 present the same
two input strings in opposite order, with different original producer experiment
references. Their two reused answers preserve that duplicated witness; it is not
two independent failures. There are 35 registered pairs and 34 unique request
pairs. This overlap is explicit rather than hidden by a changed scorer or an
edited frozen source.

One reverse-orientation Bread chain contradicts its tested shortcut; there are
no forward-orientation chain witnesses. The chain shares the Bread reversals,
so its count is not an additional independent semantic error. All 35 reciprocal
pairs have settled answers. The mixed report's overall `complete` flag remains
false because the scorer deliberately excludes the 32 snapshots, not because
cases are unrun.

Concrete current witnesses include:

- Isolated 008: Food/Coffee checks nourishment in one orientation and accepts
  consumption alone in the other.
- Isolated 012 and 022: Bread/preparation and Bread/bagel-making switch between
  food-kind classification and information about preparing that food.
- Isolated 013: Bread's meal-component aspect establishes containment in one
  orientation, while its unrestricted food-kind scope blocks it in the other.
- Isolated 016: Processed food/Food dishes disagrees over whether prepared foods
  and combinations necessarily meet the alteration requirement; the preferred
  individual label remains qualified.
- Isolated 018 and 020: Salsa and pie-preparation relationships disagree across
  orientations while meal-use and activity boundaries receive different readings.
- Isolated 025 and 026: Eating and the author's coffee alternate between Food's
  nourishment-restricted meaning and a more general consumption interpretation.

The original snapshot answers now consistently reject the edible-material Food
relationships to appliances, cookware and stockpots. They also reject the
consumption-only Food bridge beneath nourishment-restricted Food. This single
draw does not establish a causal repair by any particular prompt sentence or a
reliability estimate; the historical Compare-v48 run is not a fresh matched arm.

Accepted snapshot qualifications remain for Butter's optional role, Salsa,
pie/bagel/sourdough preparation and Eating. Meals also accepts the original Food
dishes branch without addressing its broader Bread/Salsa descendants. These
require whole-scope review; an isolated Meals/Food dishes positive has no such
supplied descendant conflict. The six provisional positive controls and two
`neither` controls agree with their expectations. Processed food's changed
snapshot label is not automatically an error against the older provisional
`uncertain` expectation.

Compare-v69 remains experimental. This panel exposed unresolved comparison
mechanisms despite preserved positive controls. Its role-condition and
kind/activity interpretation switches motivated the subsequent diagnostic
drafts and v75 qualification below.

## Independent answer review

Two independent agents reviewed disjoint portions covering all 102 answers.
Their assessments are recorded through native `review --role agent`: **76 accept,
20 reject and six uncertain**. These are qualified agent assessments, not new
human labels or an accuracy estimate. Four rejects concern the reason alone
(`isolated-020-reverse`, `isolated-021-reverse`, `isolated-023-forward` and
`isolated-023-reverse`): their `neither` labels are defensible because meal use is
not established, but their categorical activity/food exclusion is inconsistent
with the information-topic comparison convention.

The six uncertain assessments preserve two unresolved producer boundaries:
Processed food's alteration condition and Eating's human/nourishment scope.
Each has one original snapshot and two isolated orientations. A reversal can be
a definite comparison inconsistency even when the preferred individual label is
not settled. Duplicate inputs and overlapping chains remain explicit; rejected
answers are not independent failure counts.

After recording reviews, native `show --json` verified that all 102 input hashes,
parsed outputs and producer receipts remained unchanged. The initial 85 relevant
production-path tests for pair preparation, snapshots, consistency and settled
relationships passed. Both dataset reviews also verified the original input
mapping and reciprocal producer identities. No review required another model
call, changed either prompt or altered the original hierarchy.

The post-landing rerun passed 84 tests and hit the existing 30-second subprocess
timeout in
`test_ordinary_parent_checks_settled_pair_excluded_by_same_note`. That constructor
integration test passed when rerun alone without source or timeout changes.
The full-suite timeout remains a verification limitation; its cause was not
established. These tests use the local offline transport fixture and made no
teacher calls. Native result, review and receipt validation still passed after
landing.

## Compare-v70: counterexample diagnostic draft

The user approved drafting separate counterexample questions and answers for
both containment directions. [Compare-v70](Hierarchy-compare-v70.md) retains
v69's fixed-meaning and information-topic rules and adds two concrete questions,
linked answers and concise justifications. Its native revision recipe and
receipt use the recorded last-approved Compare-v48 baseline; the earlier v69
changes remain visible in that full-context delta.

Each question tests whole-topic coverage, including equal scope. A valid
counterexample must be admitted by the child's fixed meaning and excluded by
the parent's. Positive coverage requires a whole-scope justification; failing
to find a counterexample is insufficient. An unresolved answer is null and
requires `uncertain`. Canonical branch checks retain their existing consequences
and may independently prevent a settled relation. Diagnostics do not choose
new boundaries or change meanings.

This is an **unrun draft**, not an adopted prompt or a demonstrated improvement.
No validation cases were inserted as prompt examples. Its output adds
`questions` and `answers` to `meaning`, `relation` and `reason`; the existing
three-field execution schema and constructor were not changed. Native revision,
exact reconstruction and placeholder checks make no teacher calls. Qualification
requires a matching five-field schema before any execution.

Current main's runtime integration conflicts with twenty older research adapter
and test files in this worktree. The attempted synchronization was aborted,
leaving those source files unchanged. Prompt baselines match main byte-for-byte;
only the prompt draft, its revision metadata and this maintained catalogue are
eligible for bounded integration. This worktree remains needed for its unmerged
research support and preserved native experiment artifacts.

At the user's request, [Compare-v71](Hierarchy-compare-v71.md) is a draft-only
revision against exact v70 bytes. It removes source `context` fields from local
entries, original ancestors and canonical supporting entries, and removes their
uses from the comparison instructions. Fixed meanings continue to establish
scope. Original posts, subjects and canonical branch checks remain in this
bounded revision. The counterexample diagnostics and five-field output are
unchanged. No input adapter, runner, Bootstrap prompt or hierarchy was changed;
no teacher calls were made and this version is not adopted.

## Compare-v72–v73: terminology audit drafts

The user requested consistent naming throughout the comparison prompt.
[Compare-v73](Hierarchy-compare-v73.md) is the final unrun terminology draft
against v71. `subject` names a topic; the fixed `meaning` describes the
information covered; that information is the node's scope. The comparison
outcome consistently uses `relation`. Local ancestors and canonical branches
are distinguished explicitly, and proposed parent/child roles refer to nodes,
not meaning strings. Equivalence compares covered information rather than
requiring identical wording.

The audit removes the duplicate meaning definition, replaces heading and
definition aliases, and clarifies node containment in the canonical
consequences. Existing restrictions, uncertainty rules, diagnostics, field
names, relation values and context-free input remain in place. Independent
read-only review informed the terminology fixes. Exact native recipe/receipt
reconstruction, placeholder and output-field checks require no teacher calls.
No runner, adapter, Bootstrap prompt or hierarchy changed.

V72 is preserved as the first draft. The final audit found one remaining
"label" alias and several node/scope wording mismatches; v73 records their
correction without overwriting v72. Both retain native revision recipes and
receipts pinned to v71. V73 supplies the exact baseline for v74; these
intermediate drafts were not executed. No improvement in model reliability
is claimed.

## Compare-v74: shared node data and no original evidence

The user approved a draft simplification after v73: both compared nodes have

The canonical node retains its ID, immediate parent IDs and recursive canonical

is outside that hierarchy.

The draft removes original hierarchy definitions and source-evidence conflict
checks. Comparison uses fixed meanings, their unresolved ambiguities and the
supplied canonical branches. Subjects cannot add restrictions or settle an
ambiguous meaning. Source validation remains outside this comparison contract;
original producer evidence and native receipts are preserved.

Native revision metadata is pinned to exact v73 bytes. The counterexample
diagnostics, five-field output and canonical implication checks remain
unchanged. Independent read-only review and mechanical reconstruction checks
were completed without teacher calls. At this checkpoint v74 was an unrun,
unadopted draft: the existing input adapters, runtime output schema,
Bootstrap prompts and hierarchy artifacts were not changed. Qualification must
first project saved regression inputs to this new contract through the shared
native workflow while retaining their producer provenance outside model input.

## Compare-v75: consolidated rules and distinct diagnostics

The user approved this draft edit after reviewing official reasoning-model
prompting guidance. [Compare-v75](Hierarchy-compare-v75.md) consolidates repeated
fixed-meaning, interpretation and relation-selection instructions from exact
v74 bytes. `answer` supplies the counterexample or coverage conclusion;
`justification` supplies its brief decisive basis. The output fields and question
IDs are unchanged.

Both directional tests still include equality. Proper containment requires
established failure in the reverse direction; any null answer requires
`uncertain`. Interpretation ambiguity must leave the affected coverage answer
unresolved even if a separate canonical conflict already makes the final
relation uncertain. A branch conflict can prevent a relation without changing
settled answers about the selected meanings. All recursive canonical implication
checks and the source-free shared node contract remain in place.

Independent read-only review found the answer-level ambiguity requirement during
consolidation; it was retained in the global ambiguity instruction before native
revision. Exact recipe reconstruction, native receipt hashes, input placeholder,
diagnostic field names and preserved semantic obligations were checked without
teacher calls. At this checkpoint v75 was an unrun, unadopted draft. Runtime
qualification was subsequently authorized and completed below; the prompt
itself remains unchanged and no reliability improvement is claimed.

### Consistency and semantic review

All 35 registered reciprocal witnesses are settled, representing **34 unique
request pairs**. Native reversal checking identifies **three distinct
conflicts**: isolated 014 (Butter/Food preparation), 024 (sourdough preparation/
Food), and 025 (Eating/Food). The 008/033 duplicate is consistent and remains
explicitly reported. No within-orientation transitivity, cycle or equivalence
conflict is detected among the supplied isolated comparisons. No response
declares equivalence, so equivalence substitution is not substantively exercised
by this draw. Missing comparisons remain unknown. The report's overall
`complete: false` is due to the 32 snapshots excluded from the isolated scorer,
not unfinished execution.

Two independent reviewers assessed disjoint sets covering every output, using
only v75's model-visible fixed meanings, canonical branches and diagnostics.
Every counterexample was assessed for admission by the proposed child's whole
meaning and exclusion by the proposed parent's meaning; positive conclusions
were assessed for whole-scope coverage and recursive canonical consequences.
Native `review --role agent` records contain **76 accept, 17 reject and nine
uncertain** verdicts. These are agent assessments of responses, including
diagnostics, not human gold or an accuracy estimate. Rejected responses can
share an underlying pair or retain a defensible final label.

Concrete Compare failures are:

- **One fitting alternative replaces whole-scope coverage.** Butter says
  "used as a food, spread or cooking ingredient." `isolated-014-forward` accepts
  full coverage under Food preparation because one ingredient role fits. Its
  reversed answer correctly finds direct eating outside preparation.
  `snapshot-014-c005` repeats the same coverage error.
- **Parent conditions disappear in positive answers.** Pie/bagel preparation
  acquires an unstated meal role (`snapshot-020-c020`, `snapshot-021-c020`, both
  isolated 020 orientations). Sourdough preparation and Eating acquire Food's
  human-nourishment condition (`snapshot-024-c001`, `snapshot-025-c001`, isolated
  024/025 forward); reversed answers supply counterexamples outside that condition.
- **Endpoint coverage changes when branches appear.** `snapshot-009-c001`
  excludes cultivation from the consumption-based Food topic, whereas both
  isolated 009 answers recognize it as information about the same covered food.
  Branch evidence may prevent a final placement, but does not change endpoint
  meanings or their coverage answers.
- **Physical-kind exclusion substitutes for information-topic exclusion.**
  `snapshot-004-c005`, `isolated-004-forward`, both isolated 011 orientations and
  `isolated-021-forward` reject preparation because the activity is not itself
  the edible material or finished dish. That alone is not a counterexample
  under v75's information-topic convention. A human-use or meal-use boundary
  may independently justify the same `neither` label; the faulty diagnostic does
  not certify a wrong label.
- **A required canonical ancestor check is skipped.** In `snapshot-022-c068`,
  Bread can cover bagel-making information at the endpoints, but ancestor C020
  Food dishes requires meal service/consumption. The reason asserts that bagels
  are eaten as part of a meal without that condition in the local meaning.
  The endpoint answers can remain settled while the incompatible branch
  requires an `uncertain` relation.

The nine uncertain reviews preserve three unresolved boundary families, each
represented by a snapshot and both isolated orientations: Coffee's intended
consumer, Processed food's alteration boundary, and the particular author's
coffee purpose. In isolated 016, manufactured flour retained outside meal
service is a credible witness against Food dishes covering all Processed food.
The opposite raw-fruit witness does not settle whether combining or arranging
otherwise unchanged fruits counts as alteration through preparation. A settled
preferred label is not supplied by this review. Nor may a hypothetical purpose
be asserted as a fact about the particular author's coffee. The generic Coffee
008/033 definition is a different scope, not a repeated draw of that case.

A third read-only reviewer independently overlapped seven hard outputs:
Butter's two directions, Processed food/Food dishes' two directions, and
snapshots 004, 012 and 022. That reviewer likewise rejected Butter's one-role
coverage, the physical-type diagnostic and the meal-ancestor approval; accepted
the narrower Bread/preparation information relationship; and preserved the
raw-fruit boundary qualification. These overlapping assessments are not extra
case trials or additional independent error counts.

The positive fatigue/sleepiness and explicit-dinner controls remain supported
on semantic review. The two model `uncertain` relations, snapshots 019 and 023,
identify canonical descendant conflicts; their selected endpoint coverage
answers remain settled. This demonstrates that branch uncertainty can be
reported without rewriting the endpoint meanings, but does not excuse the
missed ancestor check above.

### Verification and next decision

Inspection identified a diagnostic defect shared by all three reversal
families: the forward `canonical_contains_local` question tests a positive
connection rather than a concrete possible exclusion from the parent's fixed
scope. The user specifically asked whether Butter's questions were supposed
to seek counterexamples and requested preserving this finding.

Exact saved questions:

- `isolated-014-forward`: "Does butter's fixed meaning establish a role in
  food preparation, rather than merely identifying a finished food?" This
  checks that one role fits, without testing Butter's other admitted uses.
- `isolated-024-forward`: "Is making bread with a sourdough starter, including
  its fermentation process, covered by food preparation?" This checks generic
  preparation rather than Food's required human-nourishment scope.
- `isolated-025-forward`: "Does Food cover consuming food by mouth and
  swallowing it, including eating gamey meat and enjoying or disliking it?"
  This repeats the positive consumption connection without probing Eating's
  scope outside Food's required human-nourishment conditions.

V75 already requires a concrete question that could reveal information admitted
by the proposed child and excluded by the proposed parent. It does not require
negative grammatical wording: "Does the parent cover [a concrete admitted
child case]?" can be a valid counterexample test. The defect is substituting
positive association or a matching role for that test. These are additional
diagnostic defects within the already-rejected outputs, not three additional
independent failures. An independent read-only reviewer confirmed the 024/025
question defect. At that inspection stage, preferred individual labels were
qualified agent assessments, without human semantic gold; this does not prove
which prompt wording caused the failures. Prompt, inputs and saved outputs
remain unchanged.


The user subsequently approved **neither** for the exact Preparing sourdough
bread / Food meanings in `isolated-024-forward` and `isolated-024-reverse`.
The approved explanation was: "Under these definitions, neither is correct in
both orientations." The user's verbatim reply was: "\"Under these definitions,
neither is correct in both orientations.\" agree". Food requires intended human
consumption, while the supplied sourdough-preparation meaning also admits bread
made solely for animal feed. This is a user-approved expectation for this exact
pair, not a general prohibition on Food containing preparation activities.

The user also approved **neither** for the exact Eating / Food meanings in
`isolated-025-forward` and `isolated-025-reverse`. The approved explanation was:
"the positive answer overlooks Food’s human-consumption requirement. Eating’s
meaning also permits a cow eating livestock-only fodder, which Food excludes."
The user's verbatim reply was: "gotcha agree." This expectation applies to the
supplied meanings in this pair. Butter's preferred label remains an agent
assessment. Native outputs, attributed agent reviews and the prompt are
unchanged. Present each reviewed pair in isolation and explain why its saved
judgments constitute a failure.

Every case has exactly one current native agent review. After review, all 102
input strings/hashes, raw outputs, parsed values and receipt references remained

62-test production-path preparation, diagnostic-validation and consistency
suite passed with `tiktoken` supplied. Native support and prompts were landed

tests. No production scheduling code was changed. The real c32 experiment was
prepared and completed before that shared release landed; its historical v1

does not imply that concurrency remains configurable in the new runner.

**V75 is tested but not qualified for production adoption.** The historical
v69 run had nine distinct reversals versus three in this v75 draw. This is not
a matched causal comparison: prompt wording, visible input and output contract

The user authorized a focused draft, [Hierarchy-compare-v76](Hierarchy-compare-v76.md),
against approved v75. It replaces two paragraphs in the containment diagnostic:

not adopted. This is a semantic hypothesis, not an
established cause or fix for the v75 errors. Its brief diagnostic requirements

### Approved v76 interpretation diagnostic

The user authorized isolating the unsupported interpretation in the sourdough
answer. This diagnostic preserves v76 and all Bootstrap-v96 / ASv6.100 meanings;
it does not revise the production prompt, regenerate meanings or build a graph.

The frozen schedule is eight fresh unchanged v76 responses and eight supplied
candidate-membership probes: both orientations of 024, 025 and 034, isolated
022 forward, and snapshot 022. The two bagel inputs are an isolated endpoint
control and a graph-bearing ancestor check, not reciprocal snapshots. Each
generic diagnostic imports exact inputs and review-only evidence from the pinned
completed v76 gate. Saved comparison answers do not enter the diagnostic input.

Four versioned `Compare-candidate-*-v1` templates supply deliberately selected
hypotheses: animal-feed-only sourdough, a dog swallowing dog-only food,
pantry-bagel preparation for human nourishment solely as non-meal snacks, and
the design/installation of a cooking-role induction hob with an integrated
extractor. These are experimental challenge stimuli, not production examples,
new Bootstrap targets or a representative accuracy dataset. The appliance
stimulus guards against rejecting information about an activity's established
equipment role solely because it concerns a different physical kind.

Each probe separately tests admission beneath the local node, the selected
canonical node, and all supplied canonical ancestors, with a concise decisive
basis. Human-only Food must exclude animal-only use; the generic sourdough and
Eating meanings admit those hypotheses. Bagel preparation and Bread admit the
non-meal snack hypothesis, whereas the meal-restricted Food dishes ancestor
excludes it. Role-defined appliance information should remain covered under
the unchanged Compare information-topic convention. Independent review will
check these hypotheses and retain any disagreement rather than manufacture gold.

Run ceilings total 33 actual attempts: 17 for the fresh eight-case v76 draw
(including warmup/retries) and four per two-case candidate probe. The existing
20,000 counter remains authoritative. Sol 6.1 medium and the main shared runner
remain fixed. V76 retains its native cached SSE configuration; short diagnostic
templates run uncached rather than receiving artificial cache-padding text.

If v76 reproduces an error but the supplied-candidate check correctly admits
the child and excludes the parent, that demonstrates capability in the narrower
validation task and a failure to preserve/use that boundary during full Compare.
It does not distinguish search from positive-coverage proof conclusively, prove
which prompt sentence caused the error, or certify a fix. If the probe also
fails, the unsupported interpretation persists during explicit candidate
validation. Non-reproduction of a fresh v76 error does not erase the earlier
witness. No automatic production edit or adoption follows from either outcome.

Each request had one HTTP attempt, with no prior response/conversation ID or

are independent draws; cached instructions do not make them shared conversations.

The fresh v76 draw has two reviewed failures:

- **024 forward:** it again asserts human nourishment as a requirement of the
  sourdough activity. Reverse now finds a display-only counterexample and
  returns neither. The error changed orientation; it did not disappear.
- **Snapshot 022:** endpoint Bread/bagel containment remains supported, but
  its reason again inserts meal use to clear the Food dishes ancestor. The
  selected endpoint answers can remain settled while the relation is uncertain.

Eating now returns the approved neither in both orientations; both appliance
controls and isolated Bread/bagel containment remain positive. Three complete
reciprocal pairs yield one reversal witness, for 024. Isolated bagel reverse was
not scheduled, and the graph snapshot is not a reciprocal case. These bounded
draw counts are not a reliability estimate. Independent review accepts six
v76 responses and rejects two. Several positive-direction questions remain
general coverage probes rather than named concrete counterexample candidates;
accepted relations do not certify their question form.

The supplied animal-feed sourdough and dog-only consumption candidates are
correctly admitted by their activity meanings and excluded by human-only Food
in both orientations. The bagel probes correctly admit snack-only preparation
at both endpoints; the graph snapshot separately admits it beneath Food and
excludes it beneath meal-restricted Food dishes. All required nodes are checked
exactly once, including both snapshot ancestors. These six responses receive
independent accept reviews.

The two appliance probes instead reject Food preparation membership. Independent
qualification leaves those outcomes uncertain: the abbreviated diagnostic
omitted v76's explicit established-equipment-role rule and used a narrower
activity-only convention. V76 itself retained the role-based positive in both
orientations. This is an exposed diagnostic semantic confound, not evidence
that the production Compare prompt failed those appliance comparisons.

**Finding:** the model can recognize the disputed exclusions when given concrete
candidates, yet the full v76 task still asserts unsupported conditions while
justifying full coverage or clearing ancestors. That supports targeting positive
coverage justification and consequence checking rather than attributing every
failure to an inability to understand the words. It does not conclusively
separate counterexample discovery from coverage proof or identify a responsible
prompt sentence. The diagnostic also changes output task, instruction length
and message placement: cached v76 uses developer/user messages, while uncached
probes use a user message. No production improvement is claimed from that
multi-factor comparison, and the shorter diagnostic is not a replacement prompt.

All 16 outputs have attributed native agent reviews. Generation and reviewed
snapshots preserve original input, evidence, output, parsed values, usage and
receipts; no old answers were supplied to new calls. V76 and producer meanings
were not edited; no Bootstrap work, hierarchy construction, external message or
conditional full-panel run occurred. The next proposed controlled change is to
require a positive coverage justification to establish the parent's required
conditions from the child's own meaning, preserving the existing information-
topic/activity-role rules. That proposal remains untested and unadopted.

### Condition diagnostic v1: preregistered bounded test

The user authorized the proposed explicit condition diagnostic after v77's
failed gate. This is a separate experimental task/schema, not a production
Compare edit or adoption. `Compare-condition-diagnostic-v1.md` is natively
revised from the tested v77: its complete node definitions, fixed-scope rules,
information-topic convention, activity-role rules and state distinctions remain
byte-identical. The procedure/output now records each required parent phrase,
supporting child phrase or necessary implication, and established,
counterexample or unresolved status. Required canonical consequences use the
same records. Optional/descriptive statements must not become mandatory child
conditions. The two-field schema is deliberately separate from the production
five-field Compare contract; no adapter, constructor or Bootstrap change is
needed or authorized by this experiment.

The generic native prepared-experiment import pins the v77 gate envelope at
`b99490e3d0913cc16e8eadd650112aefc9a9d08ed2be26600ba0ee323ecbfe4b`.
Its exact ASv6.100/Bootstrap-v96 meanings and source-free `INPUT_JSON` remain
fixed; original producer receipts and assessment evidence remain review-only.
This is historical producer qualification data, not certification of latest
Bootstrap. No saved answer or expected relationship enters model input.

The staged schedule is fixed before inference:

1. Initial eight: 024 and 025 in both orientations, 034 in both orientations,
   isolated 022 forward, and snapshot 022/C068. Each direction must settle the
   actual required boundary without adding child restrictions; the appliance
   and isolated bagel positives must survive. Snapshot 022 must retain endpoint
   coverage but identify the unmet meal condition of Food dishes and return
   uncertain. Stage cap: 17 actual attempts including warmup/retries.
2. Only if all eight labels and condition records pass independent review,
   run the other seven fixed v77-gate cases: 006 both, 014 both, 022 reverse
   and 031 both. Stage cap: 15 attempts.
3. Only if all 15 pass, repeat the initial eight with native `--repeat` in a
   fresh experiment using the identical diagnostic prompt and settings.
   Stage cap: 17 attempts. Repeated requests must have distinct response IDs
   and no conversation linkage; cache hits do not imply answer reuse.

Maximum dispatch allowance for this protocol is 49 attempts, within the existing
persistent 20,000 counter. Independent cases continue within a dispatched stage;
a failure stops dependent stages, rather than selecting favorable responses.
Shared runner, subscription Sol 6.1 medium, SSE and automatic prefix caching
remain fixed. Current shared queue chooses concurrency automatically (30 at
preparation); no private execution or fresh spending allowance is introduced.

Native schema validation is not semantic validation. Review must separately
check exact phrase quotations, both endpoint directions, completeness of required
ancestor/descendant checks, permissive versus required clauses, every child
alternative, valid admitted-but-excluded counterexamples and relation consistency.
Necessary topic implications may establish coverage without a literal matching
phrase; physical kind mismatch alone may not reject an established activity-role
topic. Existing human judgments are retained with their exact scope; other
controls are provisional agent assessments, not human gold.

This structured-output experiment tests whether explicit condition attribution
exposes unsupported inference. It changes procedure and schema together, so an
improved draw would not identify one causal sentence, prove stable reliability,
or justify production adoption. No validation examples are added to the prompt.
Official [reasoning guidance](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/reasoning-best-practices)
supports direct instructions and concise requested diagnostics rather than
reasoning traces; it does not establish the semantic convention or this fix.

### Completed condition diagnostic v1

The initial eight cases completed validly: **nine actual attempts** including
one warmup, no retries, failures, reuses or missing usage. All eight comparisons
reported cache hits. Native API-equivalent cost, including warmup, was
**$0.0555016**; subscription incremental cash remained zero. The shared
persistent counter was preserved; these calls are attributed by receipts, not
by subtracting a counter concurrently used by other agents. The separate native
usage source is `compare-condition-diagnostic-usage-v1.json`; its output is
`target/compare-condition-diagnostic-v1/usage-v1.json`.

Independent semantic review accepts five outputs and rejects three. Six of
eight final labels are supported, but one of those six has an incorrect
condition record. These counts are bounded agent assessments with exact human
judgments preserved where available, not a human-gold accuracy estimate:

| Cases | Result | Assessment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 024, both orientations | Food contains sourdough preparation | Reject: both conflict with the user's exact neither judgment by inventing intended human nourishment. |
| 025, both orientations | Neither | Accept: cattle-only/horse-only consumption defeats human-nourishment Food; the optional preferences sentence is not made a human-eater requirement. |
| 022 isolated forward | Bread contains bagel preparation | Accept: preparation concerns cooked flour-based dough; no human-consumer or meal restriction is present in this isolated Bread meaning. |
| 034, both orientations | Food preparation contains cooking appliances | Accept: the established cooking role and listed device aspects survive the information-topic convention. |
| Snapshot 022/C068 | Uncertain | Correct final label and meal counterexample, but reject the diagnostic's unsupported human-nourishment implication at Food ancestor C001. |

The repeated semantic mistake is now explicit. Both sourdough directions quote
the parent's intended-human-nourishment condition and quote the child's
`making bread`, then claim:

> Bread is a food intended for people to eat for nourishment.

The child wording establishes the bread-making activity and fermentation
method, not this required intended consumer or purpose. The exact previously

objection is an agent assessment, not new human gold.

consistently incorrect sourdough positive. Consistency alone does not establish
correctness. The isolated/snapshot contrast is not a reversal pair.

### Condition diagnostic v2: preregistered purpose-variation test

The user authorized the next focused test. V2 inserts one paragraph into the
tested diagnostic v1. For each required parent purpose or role, the paragraph
asks whether it can differ or be absent while information still satisfies the
child's entire fixed meaning; the existing `basis` records the variation and
result. Excluding a candidate must be supported by child wording or a necessary
implication requiring that parent condition. A customary purpose alone is
insufficient. Unsettled admission/exclusion remains unresolved.

All other prompt bytes, the two-field output schema and inference settings stay
unchanged. This is a diagnostic procedure change, not production Compare
adoption. In particular, the existing information-topic/activity-role convention
remains fixed: varying actual use does not erase an explicitly required design
purpose. Rejecting an invalid candidate is not itself proof of coverage; the
child's meaning must establish the parent's condition across its whole scope.

The first native experiment inherits the completed v1 initial eight through
`prepare --from`, overriding only prompt/path/ID. Inputs, review-only producer
evidence and schema remain exact. Prior responses are baseline evidence only;
none enters model input. Independent preflight must verify this before dispatch.
No `--repeat` is needed for the first new-prompt draw.

The conditional schedule is the same fixed 8 + 7 + fresh 8 used for v1:

3. Only after all 15 pass, prepare a new native `--repeat` of the v2 initial

Maximum dispatch for this protocol is 49 actual attempts inside the existing
20,000 persistent allowance. A failed dispatched stage still completes its
independent cases; later stages remain unprepared. The shared runner's automatic

No Bootstrap, adapter, constructor or hierarchy work is included.

and necessary implications; a condition neither stated nor implied may vary,

than another counterexample-question format. It does not expand a participant
topic to all information about that participant, erase lexical implications,
force negative labels or remove valid role/aspect connections. Canonical-conflict
rules and unresolved coverage remain unchanged.

### V99 failed qualification and independent expectation challenge

All14 outputs complete validly in16 fresh attempts including two warmups, with

A separate reviewer examined the exact inputs and V97's convention before seeing
any outputs, reviews or historical findings. Nonhuman alertness is a strong
admitted child case: mental wakefulness, attention and readiness do not establish
a person's mind ([canine attention research](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC3916763/)).
A deliberately designed mouse ketogenic feeding practice likewise preserves very
low carbohydrates, relatively high fat and the ketosis purpose, while human
researcher agency does not make the consumer a person
([mouse ketogenic feeding](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC6024661/),
[mouse ketone metabolism](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/35995402/)).
These are agent semantic assessments supported by factual admission evidence,
not new human gold or a rule that all unstated conditions are variable.

The same independent challenge qualifies018 and041. Whether making edible
bagels solely for destructive testing remains a "food-preparation activity"
depends on whether that phrase itself requires an eating purpose. Likewise,
animal food abstention does not suffice for041: the child requires deliberate
abstention as a dietary practice. Observed dogs choosing to wait with food
accessible establish behavioral possibility, not that category membership
([canine delayed food choice](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5341119/)).
Both are lexical scope qualifications; neither prior positives nor provisional
negatives settle them. Earlier reviews are preserved, with this later challenge
recorded separately. Removing a contradiction does not establish accuracy.

outputs:038/042 both and019-reverse are rejected;018/041 both are qualified;

copies and native receipts pass. This later qualification does not rewrite
prior-version reviews or input bytes.

### V100: counterexample construction instead of a yes/no coverage question

V100 changes only V97's question-generation paragraph. Each diagnostic asks

in this contrast. Scope rules, canonical implications, meanings, field order
and production schema remain unchanged.

collection; excluding them from this mechanism gate does not resolve or discard

candidates were actually produced. Better labels without valid proofs, or
loss of activity-role/aspect positives, do not qualify the prompt. This contrast
can test candidate construction versus abstract classification, but cannot by
itself separate that mechanism from reduced lexical priming.

The next diagnostic supplies hypothetical nonhuman alertness and ketogenic
feeding candidates without an expected answer. Compare-counterexample-diagnostic
V1 describes their properties only; V2 adds their familiar category names.

unchanged. The four native requests are038-forward/042-forward in both prompt
conditions, with caps5 and5 under the existing counter. Both templates are
diagnostic interventions, never adoption candidates, validation successes or
synthetic examples for the reusable Compare prompt. The model must assess
admission, exclusion and the relation rather than accept the supplied witness.

Label-dependent admission would support taxonomy sensitivity. Correct admission
in both conditions while ordinary comparison fails would show that supplied
candidates can overcome the error, without separating search from lexical
priming. Correct admission with a positive relation would identify proof/label
inconsistency. Identical failures would not rule out priming from the unchanged
meaning strings. Independent ordinary-gate qualification remains necessary.
Independent pre-run review confirms that only candidate naming changes between
the templates. Both selection instructions still name the child topics, and
supplied entity bindings/exclusion stipulations can overcome defaults; the test
does not remove all taxonomy cues or isolate binding from topic-operator effects.

witness does not establish exclusion from "involving a person's thought," which

forced as the replacement label. The accepted negative witnesses test decisive
conditions even where the diagnostic question still quotes a compound phrase.

does not establish a negative. This targets choosing an already shared
condition instead of the missing restriction, rather than another general
identity warning or a supplied validation witness. Independent review finds
no semantic blocker to the controlled contrast.

### V112/V113: separate alternative coverage from reading selection

Two controlled candidates branch independently fromV110. V113 makes conditions
explicitly local to each parent alternative, requires coverage through at least
one alternative/connection for every case within every child alternative, and makes the question
name the alternative being challenged. Escaping one alternative is insufficient;
the candidate must preserve every child requirement and escape the others too.
It removes the instruction to change only one condition because escaping
different alternatives can require multiple unrestricted features to vary.
Identity priority, meanings, topic operators and canonical guards remain intact.
The unrunV111 draft had a quantifier precision concern: different parent
alternatives may cover different cases within a single child alternative.
V113 explicitly permits that union coverage before any inference.

V112 changes only the ambiguity paragraph. It tests the same concrete candidate
under competing ordinary readings of the complete meaning, identifies the
decisive phrase and settles readings through wording, necessary implications
and settled usage. If material competing readings remain viable, use null.
An omitted qualifier alone is not ambiguity. This targets silent selection
of roast, broth, eating-purpose or morning-context boundaries, not a policy
of rejecting named kinds or making every extra qualification uncertain.

necessary language implications. V116 is an unrun intermediate draft whose

after establishing deliberate design remain competing explanations. Test
whether actual proofs retain person restrictions; coherent labels or a

### V119 versus V121: ordinary-variant wording or demonstrations

Two independent interventions start from V118; they are not combined.
V119 replaces only its first necessary-implication sentence: before treating
a customary composition, method or function as necessary, test a recognized
ordinary variant preserving the meaning's kind/activity and every restriction.
A label cannot establish admission and the counterpart cannot establish
necessity. It does not specifically repair designer-versus-consumer confusion.

V121 instead retains all V118 instructions and adds two synthetic full-schema
demonstrations outside the original-post datasets. Hair cutting distinguishes
a human performer from a required human recipient. Mechanical Watches versus
general Watch repair distinguishes a recognized ordinary variant from a
narrower supplied definition, while preserving qualifying kind aspects and
activity roles. These fictional meanings are prompt illustrations, not
Bootstrap outputs or new gold. V120 is an unrun intermediate illustration;
V121 restores the internal ordinary kind name wristwatches so the second
demonstration actually tests lexical admission rather than only literal fields.

Each version freezes the same 18 core and 13 controls from V118. Initial
dispatch is 62 comparisons plus four warmups: 66 attempts absent retries/reuses,
with stage caps37/27 per version under the existing persistent counter.
Only a clean initial semantic gate justifies repeating or broadening a version;
failed and qualified proofs remain visible. If demonstrations improve outcomes,
their two contributions require separate assessment before causal attribution.
Unchanged inputs, evidence, producer receipts, output schema and execution

### V125: eliminate question bookkeeping, retain visible scope tests

The counterexample questions and separate diagnostic answers are user
requirements. Exactly one quoted condition and a mandatory variation tied to
that quotation were research devices. V122 replaces only V121's two search/
recording paragraphs. It removes that output bookkeeping while requiring the
justification to identify and resolve applicable missing identity/consumer
restrictions before other conditions. Full child admission, all parent routes,
necessary implications, canonical consequences and the five-field schema remain
unchanged. Optional quotes in the retained demonstrations remain permitted.
Incorrect questions, missing identity proofs and unsupported complete-coverage
claims remain errors; eliminating a formatting requirement is not proof that
semantic omissions are fixed.

admission testing; it does not require inventing a valid counterexample when
containment holds. V122 and its prepared experiments remain unrun. V125 is the
corrected question-procedure intervention, not a semantic-boundary change.

### V134: distinguish a failed candidate from impossible admission

V134 replaces only V133's rejected-variation paragraph. When a proposed case
violates a child requirement, test whether satisfying it preserves exclusion
from every parent alternative and permitted topic connection. Independently
evaluate the corrected case against the entire child meaning; reject only from
child wording or a necessary implication. An incomplete failed candidate does
not establish full coverage. This hypothesis targets the observed move from
rejecting non-deliberate examples to asserting a required practitioner identity;
it does not preselect an animal label or settle dietary-practice semantics.

variation. V135 remains an unrun intermediate; no illustration meaning or
relation changes, and no corpus example is added.

alone is novel or sufficient. Independent design review agrees the actual case
in the question makes a skipped variation visible and answerable.

V142 replaces only V139's question-construction paragraph. Instead of merely

violation in the question. The answer must assess admission even if a necessary
child implication is expected to rule it out. This restores an executable part
of V104's previously successful procedure without restoring quotation bookkeeping;
it is a combined presentation test, not a newly discovered semantic rule.

positive coverage, unresolved judgments and canonical guards are preserved.

kind. Shared conditions can apply to multiple alternatives when wording or
necessary implication establishes this.

### V162/V163: select the reported diagnostic after testing candidates

This independent ablation starts from V159, replacing only the sentence that
records the question. Assess candidate admission and exclusion before selecting
the reported case. An inadmissible, covered or materially ambiguous first

question reports the diagnostic or remaining unresolved case. The matching answer
still resolves that exact reported case. No changes to scope, particular-entity
identity, universal proof, canonical guards, schema or demonstrations.

production appears in some current draws but disappears in others focused on

not explicitly defer selection of the reported question. This ablation does
not presume a successful counterexample exists or settle old producer
ambiguities. A positive answer still requires the unchanged universal proof;
a later candidate must concern the same particular entity. Two independent

finds an unconditional-search duty when no applicable condition remains

### V175: directional proof outcomes instead of a repair checklist

This independent two-edit revision starts from V173, not V174. It keeps the
questions and information-connection convention, replacing the matching-answer
repair sequence, positive-test checklist and closure instructions with three
outcomes: an independently admitted/all-parent-excluded concrete case, a proof
of complete child coverage, or a null identifying a decisive unresolved boundary.
A rejected or covered candidate cannot prove universal coverage. The canonical
paragraph references these results instead of the removed outside-class
prerequisite; every ancestor/descendant obligation and decisive basis remain.

This tests procedural burden, not token count alone or a new semantic definition.
V151 shortened the complete body but retained correction and prerequisite duties;
V167 changed only repair. V175 removes compulsory repair, per-condition attempt
recording and outside-class witness prerequisites while retaining the underlying
proof obligations. Procedure-only omissions cannot be scored as errors under the
new contract. Unsupported universal claims and actual admission/exclusion gaps
remain errors. Particular entities cannot acquire hypothetical properties.

must publish question_id, justification, then answer; four fictional records
are reordered to demonstrate that sequence. Their contents, all instructions
apart from that directive, questions, input data, graph obligations and output
field sets remain identical. This differs from V96's whole-array ordering and
V170's reversal of directional records. It tests visible conclusion commitment,
not hidden reasoning order or a requested reasoning trace.

## Input-validity audit before further Compare edits

The user authorized auditing the regression inputs and separating justified
accuracy candidates from ambiguity and consistency checks. This audit makes no
model calls and changes no original meaning, expectation, output, receipt or
historical review. It does not certify human gold, withdraw an actual user
decision, or change the comparison convention.

The audited native sources are completed V104's `as100-core` and `fifty-note`
experiments under `target/compare-v104-expanded-v1`. Their experiment-envelope
plan hashes are respectively
`de8c394d36141189d2102d0ca4629391bc33dbb138a0f6e458d5c5437d52da75`
and `0a696be6670a3aa709e28c00944ccba62ae564b87b517e9019403aa9fbdfe000`.
Fresh native read-only reports are under
`target/compare-dataset-validity-audit-v1/{core-native,fifty-native}.json`.

Mechanical inspection verifies298 cases:196 core isolated requests and102
fifty-note requests, comprising70 isolated requests and32 original snapshots.
All inputs contain only the fixed local subject/meaning and canonical
subject/meaning/ID/branches contract; expectation and original-post metadata are
outside `INPUT_JSON`. All124 referenced producer experiments retain exactly
Bootstrap-v96 hash
`599e811307cfd3feac290ec91b0c763f1900cc244fccc4756d8255027a050845`.
They are ASv6.100 development inputs, not a certification of current Bootstrap.
Native inspection validates all298 saved outputs and finds no native human-role
review in these two experiments. Actual user judgments recorded elsewhere retain
their own authority and exact-input limits.

There are291 unique serialized request strings. The133 registered isolated
families represent130 distinct unordered subject/meaning pairs: core006/007,
core010/014 and fifty-note008/033 repeat the same meanings. Core009's reciprocal
requests are identical self-comparisons. Different producer receipts or pair IDs
do not turn identical wordings into independent semantic coverage. Reversal and
chain witnesses may also overlap. V176's two fictional examples contain none of
the exact regression meanings; that limited check does not prove absence of all
development-set overfitting. The entire collection is development data, with no
untouched test partition established here.

Input reviews use three separate qualifications:

- **Accuracy candidate:** the standalone meanings support an independently
  justified relationship under the established information-topic convention.
  Agent justification remains provisional, not human gold.
- **Ambiguous:** materially different supported readings of a meaning change
  the relationship. Preserve the exact disputed scope boundary. An unknown
  particular property alone is different: a clear meaning with a decisive
  unknown fact can justify `uncertain` as a provisional accuracy candidate.
  Neither situation establishes a fabricated negative relationship.
- **Consistency only:** the exact inputs can test stable interpretation, but an
  exact accuracy target depends on an unsettled information-connection convention
  or has not yet been independently justified. Record the specific missing proof;
  lack of a verified counterexample is not evidence of ambiguity or containment.
  This category does not itself establish a defect in the supplied meanings.

Canonical snapshots additionally test supplied graph consequences. Their old
edges cannot supply semantic gold; an inherited incompatible branch is distinct
from an incorrect selected pair judgment. No snapshot expectation or uncertainty
may transfer to its isolated pair. Diagnostic formatting/omissions, invalid
proofs, incorrect relations and unresolved inputs must be reported separately.

Frozen snapshot024 still carries the original provisional positive for Food over
sourdough preparation. The later user-approved neither for the exact meanings is
recorded above. Retire that obsolete provisional positive from future targets;
preserve both historical records. Neither an old expectation nor a changed
procedure should be treated as an accuracy label automatically.

Important qualifications, which must remain attached to the IDs:

- **Isolated035:** the food/meal implication and the scope of "for preparing
  meals" remain unresolved. The previously proposed containment and subsequent
  laboratory-based `neither` are historical judgments, not current targets.
  Preserve reciprocal consistency checks without prescribing either label.
- **Core018/021:** the food-preparation genus may entail eating purpose, or name
  a broader activity admitting edible display/testing output. The explicit
  eating-purpose parent must not inherit its answer from an illustration.
  Core019 lacks that genus; genuine chewy pantry bagels made solely for destructive
  testing supply a proposed counterexample. Its ingredients are not restricted
  to pantry ingredients exclusively.
- **Core022 / isolated022:** missing universal shape/production or flour proof
  does not establish the opposite relationship. A variant's name alone is not
  admission evidence. Both remain outside accuracy until the whole-child lexical
  proof or a qualifying concrete variant is established. The earlier claim that
  every unanswered ring question is a definite wrong label is not justified.
- **Core052/053:** the independently described ingredient's host can be an
  ordinary dish kind or a reference to the defined broth-specific dish. The
  old positive and the broth-free rejection are not independently certified gold.
  A fixed-meaning reversal remains instability; producer scope and Compare
  interpretation cannot be attributed exclusively from it.
- **Core069–071:** audience, contact initiation, conventional wishes and temporal
  versus referential morning context remain material. A nonhuman example alone
  does not exclude every permitted parent alternative. Do not force the familiar
  greeting positives or manufacture an opposite negative.
- **Isolated013/017/018/019/020:** Food dishes says "prepared foods or combinations
  of foods served or eaten as a meal or part of a meal." A final modifier may
  constrain both alternatives or only combinations. Another reviewer reads it
  as constraining both, so the disagreement is retained. The selected served
  meal components031/032 fit both readings. This qualification also applies to
  snapshots using that same Food-dishes meaning; old blanket meal objections
  cannot supply unconditional accuracy targets.
- **Core015 and directed-emotion/software pairs:** whole-topic coverage of tools,
  producing software or emotional reception needs an explicit convention;
  association and physical non-instance alone are insufficient. Reviewers treat
  several independent vessel/device examples as negatives by their own-topic
  scope; core015 remains conservatively qualified. The user's prior cookware
  negative is preserved, not withdrawn or replaced with an agent positive.
- **Particular coffee, desire, fatigue and dinner:** an unknown fact is distinct
  from a general-kind variant. Do not replace the particular object/person/event
  with a convenient example. An unfeeling bot fails the experienced-state child
  and cannot certify author identity. Dinner coverage at the selected dish node
  does not automatically establish the Food ancestor's human/nourishment purpose.

For isolated012, an independently recognized
[flourless bread](https://www.foodforlife.com/product/breads/ezekiel-49-sesame-sprouted-whole-grain-bread)
supports the proposed flour-restriction counterexample. For isolated022, the
[dictionary bagel definition](https://www.merriam-webster.com/dictionary/bagel)
and a [sprouted-bagel manufacturer description](https://www.foodforlife.com/product/bagels/organic-ezekiel-49-sprouted-original-bagels)
do not establish a flourless ordinary bagel satisfying every chewy/pantry
requirement. Do not transfer the bread witness to that narrower activity.

### Reassessment of the latest matched repeat

Applying the input catalogue to each version's saved52-case matched panel yields
21 provisional accuracy cases,21 ambiguous cases,3 consistency-only cases and
7 canonical snapshots. The21 provisional accuracy cases comprise16 core requests
and5 isolated controls. Native saved relations for both V173 and V176 match all21
proposed targets. This is agreement with provisional input-review assessments,
not21 independently certified correct judgments; it does not validate every
diagnostic proof or establish generalization.

The candidate's four settled reversal conflicts concern core018/053/070 and
isolated025, all classified ambiguous by the input audit. The baseline's sole
settled reversal is core070. The candidate's chain witness also includes
unqualified links. Preserve these as interpretation-instability evidence, but
do not count them as four independently established semantic errors or attribute
their cause exclusively to Compare. Supported alternate readings do not excuse
incompatible judgments after a reading has been fixed.

There is no demonstrated V176 accuracy gain on the qualified portion, and the
candidate does not improve the observed consistency counts. Further wording
changes should not be driven by forcing labels on the ambiguous remainder.
Future evaluation should retain qualified accuracy, invalid-proof checks,
interpretation consistency and canonical consequences as separate results.
This reassessment uses saved outputs only: no new model calls, producer changes,
prompt changes or rewritten historical reviews.

report is `target/compare-v176-qualified-accuracy-usage-v1.json`. Known newly
dispatched API-equivalent cost is $0.898470. Including original generation behind
reused answers gives a known $1.0782304 subtotal. Three failed attempts lack usage,
so neither complete cost nor complete caching savings is known. These estimates
are not subscription billing, and reasoning tokens are already included in output.

labels: 161/164 registered requests, or 154/157 distinct requests. Disagreements
are core005-forward (`neither` rather than provisional local containment),

isolated035-reverse (`neither` rather than provisional canonical containment).
This is comparison with pre-call agent assessments, not certified accuracy.
Targets are not changed to make this result pass.

among 78 registered/75 distinct settled reciprocal pairs. Core039 additionally
changes from unresolved to positive across orientations; the native settled-only
denominator excludes it. Both tested directional graphs have no detected chain,
equivalence-substitution or cycle conflict. Missing comparisons remain unknown;
this does not establish exhaustive consistency or general correctness.

### Qualifications exposed by output review

An additional independent read-only reviewer challenged the disputed inputs
without changing their targets or recording an extra model draw. Preserve these
qualifications alongside the pre-call results:

- **Core005:** the parent explicitly requires roughly equal milk/cream proportions;
  the child names dairy half-and-half in the author's coffee without establishing
  that formulation. The forward answer invents a 78/22 formulation for that use.
  That can test a general-use reading, but cannot establish the composition of the
  same particular product. The reverse answer instead claims the familiar product
  name necessarily imposes equal proportions. The official milkfat standard
  permits other formulations, so that necessity claim is unsupported. Particular
  versus general-use scope and the actual formulation remain qualified; neither
  original positive nor newly proposed negative is certified gold.
- **Core034/039:** experienced thinking, awareness and alertness establish a mental
  connection. Rejecting an unfeeling text generator does not establish the
  particular author's personhood. An ordinary human-author reading remains
  defensible, while a reading requiring independent identity evidence supports
  core039-forward's null. The input audit's unconditional positive targets need
  qualification, including core034's matching labels; a target match alone did
  not expose this proof gap.
- **Isolated035:** Cookware ends with "for preparing meals such as a Full English
  Breakfast." "Such as" is illustrative, but whether the preceding meal-purpose
  phrase restricts every vessel remains disputed. Under a strict purpose reading,
  Stockpots admits a vessel designed solely for preparing experimental culinary
  stock for analysis/disposal; preparing stock does not necessarily serve meals.
  The forward proof invokes the strict meal scope but tests a textile-dye vessel
  that fails the child, then assumes stock preparation establishes meal purpose.
  The reverse tests the missing purpose directly. Under an illustrative-use
  reading, that reverse case fails exclusion. The two labels switch readings;
  neither the prior blanket positive nor a replacement blanket negative is gold.

The independent product-kind check uses
[21 CFR section 131.180](https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/CFR-2025-title21-vol2/pdf/CFR-2025-title21-vol2-sec131-180.pdf):
the named milk/cream mixture is constrained by milkfat content, not an equal
ingredient-share requirement. This establishes a genuine lexical alternative,
not the particular author's product composition or a particular blend calculation.

Requalification after seeing outputs is not a measured prompt improvement.
The original selection, proposed targets, meanings, answers and receipts remain
intact. This wider test exposes interpretation/proof instability and weaknesses
in the provisional input audit; it does not justify another edit solely to force
those targets or adoption of V176.

### Historical user review of Cookware / Stockpots

The user was shown both exact meanings, V176's Cookware-contains-Stockpots
conclusion, its diagnostic question and the disputed meal-purpose inference.
The initial verbatim response was: **"i think it's fine"**. That acceptance
remains in native review history.

After inspection of the reverse diagnostic, the user explicitly accepted the
missing meal-purpose counterexample question: **"technically this was correct
unfortunately."** The user also approved the saved laboratory-only culinary-stock
vessel witness: **"this too"**. At that review, the coordinator interpreted this
as closing the boundary and derived **neither** from the laboratory vessel and
shallow frying-pan witnesses. This was a derived label, not a verbatim user label
or universal rule about cookware. The later
[current qualification](#current-cookware--stockpots-qualification) reopens that
reading; neither the laboratory admission/exclusion nor its derived label is
current semantic gold.

Native user reviews now reject `isolated-035-forward` and accept
`isolated-035-reverse`, superseding the initial acceptance while preserving its
history. The coordinator classified the forward answer as a containment failure
under that reading and the reverse as correct. Those semantic classifications
are now qualified; the emitted reciprocal relationship conflict remains. Current
agent uncertainty reviews preserve the original user-role records rather than
rewriting those earlier decisions.

The frozen meanings, original pre-call targets, outputs, agent review counts,
reversal measurement and usage remain unchanged. The earlier derived expectation
is retained as history, not current gold; its review correction is not a prompt
improvement. No inference was required for that correction.
An untouched, coverage-diverse holdout is still needed before a general accuracy
claim.

Only two answers match the user-reviewed neither relationship; ten return

internally consistent pairs nevertheless return the incorrect relationship.
The reverse orientation produces both outcomes under identical requests, so
orientation alone does not establish a deterministic cause. The observed 0/6
versus 2/6 successful searches on one pair does not establish a general effect.

Both successful answers ask specifically about meal-preparation purpose and
construct a culinary-research stockpot that simmers food solely for analysis.
They preserve the whole child and distinguish purpose from potential suitability.
All ten positive answers instead infer meal purpose from culinary stock-making,
or leave it unchecked while establishing food and heat. Three of their questions
omit meal purpose; seven combine it with shared food/heat/design conditions.
Merely including the requirement in the question therefore does not establish
that its answer tests it. Rejecting a nonfood or dye-only vessel does not close
the admitted culinary-stock variant.

The concrete observed mechanism is substituting customary use or potential meal
use for the required design purpose. A question focused on the missing purpose
coincides with the two correct outputs; this correlation does not prove that
question wording caused the success. V176 already forbids customary-function
substitution and asks for restriction-specific variants. Its additional mandatory
positive check singles out identity and consumer restrictions, so extending that
check to all applicable parent requirements is a focused wording hypothesis,
not a demonstrated fix. No new prompt version is created in this diagnostic.

qualification while preserving the child's shared conditions, and to keep the
specific identity check when testing that broader mechanism. This requires a
separate authorized revision; no additional edit or dispatch is part of V177.
