## Saved-case adjudication diagnostic V1

The user authorizes the proposed separation of constructing a counterexample
from adjudicating it. Experimental `Hierarchy-compare-case-check-v1` judges
two saved V178 concrete descriptions against both unchanged Stockpots/Cookware
meanings, in both node orders, with two fresh draws: eight case judgments.
This is a focused diagnostic prompt, not Compare-v179 or adoption of V178.
It retains the information-kind/activity convention while omitting whole-scope
proof search and canonical placement. Differences from the full prompt mean
success here cannot identify a causal wording fix by itself.

The first description comes verbatim from pair-01 reverse's laboratory-only
answer. The second is the exact commercial-kitchen noun phrase from pair-04
reverse's justification. Its appended claim of coverage is excluded. Neither
prior relations, justifications, questions, reviews nor expected answers enters
model input. Input contains only the supplied case and each node's exact subject
and meaning. Original producer diagnostics, receipts and selected excerpts stay
in review evidence; native preparation validates their frozen sources.

Pre-call agent expectations follow the user's accepted missing-purpose witness:
the laboratory-only case satisfies Stockpots and escapes Cookware's meal purpose;
the commercial meal-ingredient case satisfies both. Admission of the latter
does not establish coverage of all Stockpots. Swapping node roles must preserve
these case judgments. Missing case properties or genuine alternative scope
readings should be surfaced, rather than filled in to match expectations.
These two exposed development cases are not a held-out accuracy estimate.

The short diagnostic intentionally has no padded cache prefix. It uses the
shared runner, subscription Sol 6.1 medium, its internal queue and the existing
20,000-attempt counter. Preparation makes no calls. No original meanings,
production Compare prompt, Bootstrap prompt, historical answers or hierarchy
are changed.

The first eight judgments repeatedly exclude the laboratory-only vessel from
Cookware and admit the commercial meal-ingredient vessel under both meanings.
In both Cookware-local orders, Stockpots admission remains unresolved because
the V178 description says to simmer stock, rather than explicitly make stock
from ingredients. Preserve that genuine wording qualification. A subsequent
same-prompt diagnostic uses the original user-accepted V176 laboratory answer,
which explicitly describes preparing stock by simmering ingredients in liquid.
It tests both node orders twice: four additional attempts, with the same meanings
and no supplied expected answers. This separates the incomplete saved candidate
from interpretation of the missing meal purpose; it does not rewrite V178's
original answer or treat an unresolved admission as negative semantic gold.

### Completed saved-case diagnostic

| Saved description | Judgments | Stockpots | Cookware |

Both original explicit-case draws correctly retain the size/depth and intended
stock-making design while distinguishing laboratory analysis/disposal from meal
preparation. Both commercial draws correctly retain meal purpose despite sale
of an ingredient instead of production of a complete meal. All eight complete
case judgments survive node-order reversal. This establishes successful
adjudication of these supplied cases, not general Compare reliability.

The shorter laboratory proposal produces the same order-sensitive pattern in
both fresh draws: Stockpots-local yields a definitive fit, whereas
Stockpots-canonical yields unresolved admission. Independent review qualifies
both definitive admissions because they silently strengthen the description.
The two unresolved outputs correctly identify the production-versus-reheating
gap; all four Cookware exclusions remain sound. Do not score this as twelve
unqualified passes or reject the fully specified witness because a shorter
proposal omitted part of its design.

These findings point toward counterexample construction and unsupported
whole-scope proofs as the next production Compare mechanism to isolate. The
model can apply the relevant purpose distinction when a complete case is given.
However, this short diagnostic also changes task, prompt length, schema and
available case evidence; it does not prove which original instruction causes
the failure or that a two-stage production workflow is necessary. The current
Compare prompt remains unchanged and V178 remains unadopted. A focused next
production test should require verifying the actual absent parent restriction
in the proposed case before using it to justify coverage or exclusion, with
the existing positive and identity controls preserved.

## V179 symmetric pair draft

The user approves the audit findings about customary-case bias, unsupported
whole-scope proofs and the rigid question/search procedure, and requests
identical A/B datatypes with no parent, ancestor or node-ID input.
Experimental draft [Hierarchy-compare-v179](Hierarchy-compare-v179.md) implements
that contract through native `revise` from V176. V178 remains measured evidence;
its unsuccessful results and all original receipts are preserved.

Both `A` and `B` contain only `subject` and `meaning`. Removing the graph
metadata also removes descendants and canonical-consequence instructions.
This prompt judges the supplied pair only. Output retains questions, answers,
relation and reason, uses `A_contains_B` / `B_contains_A`, and removes the
asymmetric local-meaning copy. Fixed meanings, information-topic connections,
all-alternative coverage, particular-entity uncertainty, identity/consumer
checks and action/intention/state distinctions remain.

The draft permits coherent hypothetical counterexamples without requiring
customary use. A full-coverage justification must establish necessary coverage
of every child case, with different parent alternatives allowed for different
cases. A shared example, an inadmissible candidate or failure to find a
counterexample cannot establish that coverage. Questions identify the evaluated
boundary without prescribing a repair-and-variation sequence. The two existing
synthetic examples are adapted to the symmetric contract; no validation case
is added as an example.

At draft creation, the native comparison adapter and V176 output schema still
implement the earlier local/canonical contract and cannot execute V179 as-is.
The subsequent authorized adaptation and test are recorded below. Draft
creation is not a causal result or hierarchy adoption.
Bootstrap, frozen meanings, historical labels and hierarchy state are unchanged.
Graph consistency still requires separate evaluation; pair-only input does not
certify the consequences of insertion. No teacher calls are made for this draft.

An independent prompt review catches and resolves a draft ambiguity about
coverage by a union of parent alternatives before the version is published.
Mechanical example/contract and revision-provenance checks pass, as do all nine
existing production-path prompt-revision tests. These checks do not establish
semantic quality. A future test should retain the exact Stockpots/Cookware
meanings and identity/consumer, positive/equivalent and state controls, then
broaden qualification if the changed contract holds up. This bundled draft
cannot isolate the contribution of each wording change by itself.

| Coffee, `pair-010-forward/reverse` | Equivalent | Both definitions require brewing; ownership, morning consumption and half-and-half are absent. |
| Dietary practices/Fasting, `pair-041-forward/reverse` | Neither | An outside-human example must preserve deliberate abstention; feed withholding or involuntary starvation does not establish it. |
| Cooking/planned grilling, `pair-061-forward/reverse` | A contains B forward; B contains A reverse | The intended activity connection qualifies without asserting execution; independent indoor boiling escapes the reverse. |

The Stockpots counterexample has historical user acceptance, subsequently
reopened as recorded in the current qualification. The other four controls
remain independently reviewed agent expectations, not new human gold.
Fresh relation, question focus, case admission/exclusion and necessity proof
are assessed separately. A shared covered case does not prove universal
coverage, and consistent wrong answers do not pass. Version labels and old
expected answers are omitted from independent output review.

Initial cached preparation succeeds for V179 but refuses V180's 947-token
fixed prefix against the runner's 1,024-token minimum. It makes no inference.
The four prepared V179 directories in `target/compare-v179-examples-ablation-v1`
remain unused; cached V1 specs retain the reproduction of that preparation
diagnostic. Fresh V2 specs disable explicit prefix caching for BOTH arms, keeping
request message structure matched without padding instructions or examples.
The actual cohort uses `target/compare-v179-examples-ablation-v2` and its V2 native
usage source. Any backend-reported cached tokens remain actual usage, rather
than evidence of configured warming.

All 101 hierarchy preparation, validation and consistency tests pass on the
combined main result. Three generic/schema/report checks and the relevant UI
rerun also pass. An isolated unchanged baseline `f983f6b1`, with the same shared
runner and Python runtime, reproduces 18 failures and one error in the wider
legacy experiment harness: removed concurrency settings, scheduler/backoff/auth
expectations, receipt upgrades and missing tiktoken. The initial UI failure
passes fresh baseline and current reruns. No unrelated runner behavior is
changed to make these checks pass. Baseline evidence is retained in
`target/symmetric-adapter-baseline-f983f6b1-tests.log`.

## V179 / V180 examples ablation: results

The semantic review totals and purpose-leap assessment in this section are
historical under the subsequently reopened Stockpots expectation. The
[current qualification](#current-cookware--stockpots-qualification) separates
control assessments from unresolved Stockpots labels; emitted consistency and
usage observations below remain unchanged.

All eight V2 native experiments complete with 40 fresh valid comparisons,
zero reuse, warming, retry, failure or missing usage. Every request has one
user message, empty additional instructions and no conversation linkage;
all 40 native response IDs are distinct. The exact A/B inputs and output
schema match across arms and draws. Native `final-generation.json` snapshots
preserve each experiment before reviews. Neither frozen producer meanings nor
original ASv6.100 subjects change.

Two agents independently inspect their assigned draw without historical
targets, prompt templates or the other draw. Experiment paths disclose version
numbers, so this is not fully blinded review. Root reviews the evidence too.
One reviewer initially accepts Stockpots' purpose implication, and the other
initially accepts the human-only Fasting implication. Explicit counterexample
challenges resolve those disagreements; native attributed comments preserve
the rechecks rather than silently replacing them with pre-call targets.
Nonperson fasting witnesses are coherent hypothetical deliberate practices,
not evidence about an identified real animal.

| Measure | V179: examples | V180: no examples |
|---|---:|---:|
| Supported complete responses | 18/20 | 15/20 |
| Relation errors | 2/20 | 5/20 |
| Reversal conflicts | 2/10 | 3/10 |
| Directional labels changed between fresh draws | 0/10 | 3/10 |
| Uncertain responses | 0 | 0 |
| Native API-equivalent cost | $0.1643176 | $0.1368864 |

The ten reversal comparisons per arm are five pairs repeated twice, not ten
independent subjects. A stable wrong answer does not pass: V180's first-draw
Fasting orientations agree with each other while both ignore the person
restriction. Counts are qualified agent semantic assessments, with the
Stockpots boundary additionally supported by the user's accepted counterexample;
they are not a broad accuracy estimate.

| Pair | V179 supported / four responses | V180 supported / four responses |
|---|---:|---:|
| Stockpots / Cookware | 2 | 3 |
| Salads / Salad dressing | 4 | 4 |
| Coffee / Coffee | 4 | 4 |
| Dietary practices / Fasting | 4 | 1 |
| Cooking / Planned outdoor grilling of dinner | 4 | 3 |

Every rejected response has one unsupported directional answer; its other
direction remains supported. Questions test escape from the parent and match
the reported answer's boundary. The failure is the semantic justification,
not JSON validity or a missing counterexample question:

- **Stockpots purpose leap:** V179 `isolated-035-forward` in both draws and
  V180's second draw claim that preparing culinary stock necessarily prepares
  a meal. The same fixed Stockpots meaning permits a vessel designed exclusively
  to simmer stock for laboratory analysis and disposal. Cookware expressly
  requires meal-preparation design purpose. Stock-making, food and heat do not
  make that separate purpose unavoidable. All reverse Stockpots responses
  find a supported laboratory counterexample. In particular, V179's unchanged
  forward/reverse inputs contradict each other in both draws.
- **Fasting person restriction:** V180 `pair-041-forward/reverse` in the first
  draw and `pair-041-reverse` in the second draw assert that Fasting necessarily
  governs a person's eating, or that the broader definition adds no consumer
  restriction. The fixed Dietary practices meaning explicitly requires a
  person; Fasting does not. A hypothetical nonperson deliberately abstaining
  from available food preserves all supplied Fasting requirements. The
  positive proofs do not establish a necessary person restriction. All V179
  answers and V180's remaining orientation retain that distinction.
- **Intended cooking form:** V180's second-draw `pair-061-reverse` proposes a
  canceled outdoor dinner-grilling plan. Admission under the intended activity
  is supported, but exclusion from Cooking is not: information about an
  intended qualifying cooking form remains covered under the activity-topic
  convention without claiming any heat was actually applied. Other answers
  correctly preserve intention while comparing that form's topic.

Native usage summary is
`target/compare-v179-examples-ablation-v2/usage.json`: $0.301204 total
API-equivalent catalog cost, 40 actual attempts. This is subscription usage,
not a cash charge. Explicit prefix warming remains disabled in both arms;
four backend cache hits are retained as observed usage. The persistent shared
counter reads 18,816 immediately before launch and 18,861 after completion:
the native cohort accounts for 40 attempts, with five additional shared-counter
claims outside this cohort. Counter differences alone are not experiment accounting.

V179 is the better-supported development candidate on this small panel, but
the samples do not establish a reliable population effect of examples.
Removing examples neither fixes Stockpots nor preserves all controls.
Retain the existing examples for subsequent development and isolate the
remaining reciprocal inconsistency before broad qualification. Do not add
this validation witness as an example, claim the prompt is fixed, regenerate
meanings to erase the disagreement or adopt either version for hierarchy
construction on the basis of this panel. The A/B simplification and removal
of the former search procedure were bundled in V179; this ablation does not
identify their effects relative to V178.

## V181: challenge all applicable absent parent restrictions

The user authorizes one focused replacement in experimental
[Hierarchy-compare-v181](Hierarchy-compare-v181.md), then 20 fresh comparisons
against the preserved V179 baseline. Native `revise` from V179 changes only
the pre-positive challenge: it explicitly considers all applicable parent
restrictions absent from the child's wording, including role, purpose,
intended use and state alongside identity and consumer. A candidate must
preserve every child requirement and escape every parent alternative and
permitted information connection. Positive conclusions still require the
existing necessary whole-scope coverage rule. Particular-entity uncertainty,
performer/recipient distinctions, both synthetic examples and output schema
are unchanged.

This is an attention hypothesis, not a changed meaning of containment. V179
already states the general coverage requirement but explicitly challenges only
identity and consumer before a positive conclusion. Its two Stockpots forward
draws assume meal purpose while the reverse draws find a laboratory-only
counterexample for the identical meanings. Generalizing that focused challenge
may prevent the unsupported purpose implication; the edit does not establish
that selective emphasis caused it. Independent pre-call review verifies exact
single-edit replay, union coverage and unchanged particular-entity safeguards.

Native `prepare --from` inherits each completed V179 V2 experiment's inputs,
settings and saved baseline, with V181 as the prompt override. `--repeat`
requests independent outputs: five original pairs × two orientations × two
draws = 20 fresh comparisons. Both arms retain Sol 6.1 medium/SSE, the V179
four-field schema and disabled explicit warming. Existing shared scheduling,
native provider retry bounds and the persistent 20,000-attempt counter apply.
All meanings and source receipts remain frozen ASv6.100/Bootstrap-v96 evidence;
no Bootstrap generation or hierarchy construction occurs. The four new native
directories are `target/compare-v181-required-boundaries-v1/{pair,controls}-01`
and `-02`. The maintained usage source separates saved V179 generation from
new V181 attempts. No inference result is claimed at preparation.

The historical pre-call gate required supported Stockpots counterexamples in
both orientations (an expectation now withdrawn) and preserved positive,
equivalent, consumer and intended-state controls, with
valid questions and directional justifications. Correct labels with unsupported
proofs do not pass. Broader fixed-meaning qualification follows only if this
panel holds up; even complete success would not establish general reliability.

### Completed V181 qualification

The four native experiments complete with 20 fresh valid responses, no reuse,
warming, retry, failure or missing usage. All 20 response IDs are distinct;
requests contain one user message, empty additional instructions and no
conversation linkage. Model inputs match their saved V179 baselines byte for
byte, and schema/settings remain matched. Pre-review `final-generation.json`
snapshots preserve outputs, inputs and receipts. No producer meaning or source
evidence changes.

An independent agent reviews all 20 full responses without prescribed labels,
old answers or prompt templates; experiment paths disclose the version, so this
is not fully blinded review. Root independently checks the decisive evidence.
Attributed native reviews support 16 responses and reject four. The Stockpots
counterexample has the user's prior acceptance; the other controls remain
qualified agent assessments, not new human gold.

| Measure | Saved V179 | Fresh V181 |
|---|---:|---:|

| Relation errors | 2/20 | 4/20 |
| Reversal conflicts | 2/10 | 4/10 |

Each column represents five pairs in both orientations, repeated twice.
Reversal conflicts count the ten pair/draw checks, not ten independent topics.
Both V181 draws have identical labels, including the same two erroneous
forward answers. These small development samples do not estimate population
accuracy or establish that the edit caused the additional errors.

The four rejected responses each have one unsupported positive proof and one
supported negative case:

- **Stockpots / Cookware:** `pair-01` and `pair-02`,
  `isolated-035-forward`, return `A_contains_B`. Their proofs claim stock
  preparation "necessarily entails" or "necessarily supplies" meal-preparation
  purpose. A vessel designed exclusively to prepare culinary stock for
  laboratory analysis satisfies the entire fixed Stockpots meaning while
  lacking that Cookware purpose. Merely discarding a stock batch would not
  establish design-purpose exclusion. Both reverse answers find the complete
  laboratory-only counterexample and return `neither`. The focused wording
  change therefore does not resolve the original failure.
- **Dietary practices / Fasting:** `controls-01` and `controls-02`,
  `pair-041-forward`, return `A_contains_B`. Their proofs say deliberate
  dietary abstention necessarily governs "whether and when a person eats".
  The fixed Fasting meaning does not require a person. A coherent hypothetical
  nonhuman deliberately following its own dietary abstention practice preserves
  all its restrictions while escaping the person-specific parent scope.
  Involuntary hunger would not establish admission. Both reverse answers
  recognize that distinction and return `neither`. All four saved V179 answers
  preserve this control.

The Salads/Salad dressing, equivalent Coffee and intended-grilling controls
remain supported in all 12 responses. Questions test the reported boundary
and match their answers. The errors concern necessity claims, not JSON
validity, missing questions, a changed producer definition or a failure to
preserve the other directional counterexample. Neither lab-only vessel design
nor hypothetical nonhuman abstention assigns an unknown property to an
identified real entity.

Native accounting in `target/compare-v181-required-boundaries-v1/usage.json`
separates the 20 newly dispatched V181 attempts ($0.1711924) from the 20 saved
V179 attempts ($0.1643176). The combined $0.33551 is original catalog-equivalent
generation, not new spending. These are subscription usage equivalents, not
cash charges. Two candidate requests report backend cache hits; explicit
warming remains disabled in both arms. The persistent shared counter reads
18,861 before this cohort and 18,881 afterward. Native cohort accounting, rather
than a shared-counter difference alone, establishes its 20 attempts.

V181 fails the predeclared qualification and is not adopted or broadened.
V179 remains the better-supported development baseline, with its Stockpots
failure unresolved. Generalizing a reminder did not fix the unsupported
necessity proof. The repeatable forward/reverse asymmetry motivates a proposed
diagnostic that changes directional evaluation order while holding A/B data,
scope rules, examples and schema fixed. This could distinguish evaluation-order
effects from input-order effects; neither cause is established here. That
diagnostic is a recommendation only, with no preparation, edit or new calls
authorized by this result.

## V182: directional evaluation and report order

The user approves the proposed order diagnostic and explicitly requires
continuing the symmetric A/B prompt line. Experimental
[Hierarchy-compare-v182](Hierarchy-compare-v182.md) uses V179 as its development
baseline, rather than carrying forward V181's failed semantic reminder.
Native `revise` adds one instruction to evaluate `B_contains_A` first and
report it before `A_contains_B` in `questions` and `answers`. Each existing
synthetic example's two diagnostic arrays is reordered accordingly. All
example subjects, meanings, questions, justifications, answers and relations
remain unchanged. A/B inputs still contain only `subject` and `meaning`;
scope rules and the four-field V179 schema are unchanged.

This is an order intervention, not a one-direction task or a new containment
convention. The saved V179 Stockpots answer fails when Cookware is A and its
coverage diagnostic is reported first, but passes when it is B and that
diagnostic is reported second.
Changing requested evaluation/report order without changing A/B inputs tests
whether that difference responds to order instructions. Actual diagnostic
record order is observable; internal evaluation order is not. Instruction
wording and aligned example ordering form one intervention, so this panel
cannot separate their individual effects. Saved baseline rather than concurrent
fresh baseline also leaves time and sampling effects open.

Preparation inherits the four completed V179 V2 experiments through native
`prepare --from`, overriding only the prompt/version and requesting `--repeat`.
The same five original ASv6.100 pairs in both orientations, repeated twice,
yield 20 planned fresh comparisons. Every explicit meaning and producer
receipt remains fixed Bootstrap-v96 evidence for this controlled panel;
it does not certify newer Bootstrap versions. Both arms retain subscription
Sol 6.1 medium/SSE, the shared worker, unchanged schema and disabled explicit
warming. Shared scheduling and the persistent 20,000-attempt counter apply.
Provider-only retry bounds allow at most two attempts per case; stage caps
reserve that allowance and retries count against the same counter. The four
new directories are `target/compare-v182-direction-order-v1/{pair,controls}-01`
and `-02`. The native usage definition separates preserved V179 generation
from newly dispatched V182 attempts. No inference result is claimed here.

Qualification checks requested question/answer order, valid directional
diagnostics, complete counterexample admission/exclusion, necessary full-scope
proofs, and reversals. Independent reviewers inspect one draw each using only
exact A/B inputs and parsed outputs, without prescribed labels, producer
context or prompt templates. Required workflow documentation exposes
historical aggregate findings, so these reviews are not blinded to that history.
Stockpots' laboratory-only design witness retains its user acceptance; other
control expectations remain agent qualifications. Results must distinguish
order compliance, semantic support and repeated-label stability. Errors moving
with the first directional test would support an order hypothesis, not prove
it; errors remaining under the same A/B orientation would leave input-order
or semantic interpretation mechanisms open. No Bootstrap generation,
hierarchy construction or broader panel is included in this authorization.

## V183: synthetic function and purpose example

The following pre-call semantic gate is historical: the current qualification
withdraws its prescribed laboratory exclusion. The observed consistency and
repeat-stability failures still prevent adoption.

The user authorizes the proposed single-example experiment. Experimental
[Hierarchy-compare-v183](Hierarchy-compare-v183.md) is a native revision of V179
adding one fictional nonfood example. Existing instructions, examples, A/B input
contract, output schema and settings remain unchanged. No input-presentation
policy is adopted. Whether the added example repairs purpose inference remains
open until fresh qualification.

The added comparison separates tools designed to apply coatings for improving
appearance from tools designed to spray liquid coatings through a nozzle.
An exclusively insulating-purpose sprayer preserves the latter's whole scope
without the former's decorative purpose. An exclusively decorative bristle brush
preserves the former without a spraying design or sprayer connection. Neither
exclusion rests on current nonuse, hidden placement or failure to be a physical
instance; both retain the information-connection convention. The example does
not prohibit information about producing a qualifying kind. It contains no
validation subjects, posts or generated meanings. Independent reviews assess
the example before inference, and exact native revision replay plus all six
JSON input/output blocks validate the declared contracts.

This addresses a more specific hypothesis than the failed V177/V181 reminders
or V178 focused questions: a child's immediate function can be retained without
an inferred purpose for that function. The observed order sensitivity does not
establish this mechanism. Official
[reasoning guidance](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/reasoning-best-practices#how-to-prompt-reasoning-models-effectively)
supports direct instructions and aligned examples where needed, rather than
requesting reasoning traces; it does not establish that this example will help.
V179/V180 already supplies a zero/few-shot development comparison. The new
example adds 2,334 UTF-8 bytes to a constant-size instruction prefix, with no
corpus-size-dependent input or execution step. Native usage will account for
the increased rendered input, not only call count.

Native `prepare --from --prompt --prompt-id --repeat` inherits the same five
ASv6.100 pairs in both orientations, twice, from completed V179 experiments.
Twenty planned fresh A-first comparisons retain the frozen Bootstrap-v96
meanings and producer receipts. Four additional fresh Stockpots comparisons
inherit the completed B-first input diagnostic, preserving both orientations
and two draws. This totals 24 planned fresh requests, not 24 distinct topics.
B-first controls are not rerun, so this stage does not qualify their response
to the changed example. Saved baseline is reused as evidence, not fresh samples.

Directories are `target/compare-v183-purpose-example-v1/{pair,controls}-01`,
`-02`, and `b-first-pair-01`, `-02`. The native usage definition separates saved
A-first baseline, saved B-first Stockpots generation, and the two candidate
groups. Sol 6.1 medium/SSE, unchanged shared worker/schema, disabled explicit
warming, shared admission and the persistent 20,000-attempt counter apply.
Provider-only retry caps reserve at most two attempts per case; retry/failure
attempts count and unresolved cases stay unresolved.

Qualification requires supported full diagnostics and relations for all 24
responses, no settled reversal conflict in the 12 pair/draw checks, and no
changed relation between the two fresh draws of a fixed request. The original semantic gate required every Stockpots request to preserve the
laboratory-only stock-making counterexample or provide another admitted complete
witness. That laboratory exclusion was derived from the earlier user review
and is now reopened; it is not the current semantic gate. Other controls remain
agent-qualified. Two independent reviewers inspect one draw each, with
historical workflow exposure disclosed. Saved-versus-fresh time/provider effects
and sampling remain confounds; a clean development result would not certify
population accuracy, newer Bootstrap outputs or the entire regression set.
No Bootstrap change, hierarchy construction, broader panel or new call allowance
is included.

## Fresh matched V179 input-order diagnostic

This diagnostic holds the exact ASv6.100/Bootstrap-v96 subjects and meanings,
Compare-v179 prompt, output schema and runner settings fixed. It crosses both
A/B assignments with both physical JSON member orders, then repeats each request
once: **2 assignments × 2 member orders × 2 fresh draws = 8 requests**. It uses
no semantic gold label for Stockpots and does not alter Bootstrap or construct
a hierarchy. No new prompt version is created because its bytes are unchanged.

Native `prepare --from ... --repeat` creates `a-first-01`, `b-first-01`,
`a-first-02` and `b-first-02` under
`target/compare-v179-matched-input-order-v1`, with two original cases each.
Both orders are freshly prepared and executed under the same current code and
settings; the only intended rendered-request difference is JSON member order.
Before dispatch, verify producer identities, prompt/schema/worker bindings,
disabled explicit warming, and identical repeated request bodies. Use the
shared runner, Sol 6.1 medium, its shared admission queue and the existing
20,000-attempt counter. Each run has a four-attempt ceiling, allowing at most
one temporary-provider retry per case; actual attempts, including failures,
remain authoritative. No answer reuse or conversation linkage is intended.

Measure four reversal checks (one per order and draw), four physical-order
comparisons (one per assignment and draw), and four repeat checks (one per
assignment and order). Normalize labels to the named subjects before comparing;
physical position must not be confused with A/B identity. Inspect whether
diagnostics preserve design, food and meal boundaries without using producer
diagnostics to add restrictions. Independent reviews must keep disputed semantic
labels unresolved. Failed or unrun requests remain unresolved.

This removes the earlier saved-versus-fresh contrast from the matched comparison;
two draws still cannot establish population accuracy or isolate internal
reasoning. The native
[usage source](hierarchy-compare-v179-matched-input-order-usage-v1.json) accounts
for the eight fresh requests separately by member order. Existing control results
are not fresh controls for this diagnostic. No ordering policy or prompt revision
is adopted solely because one presentation emits a convenient label.

### Completed fresh matched order diagnostic

All eight requests complete with valid fresh outputs: no reuse, explicit warming,
retry, failure or missing usage. The four native `final-generation.json` snapshots
preserve the original answers before review. Both fresh draws reproduce the same

| Physical member order | A | B | Draw 1 | Draw 2 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A, B | Cookware | Stockpots | A contains B | A contains B |
| A, B | Stockpots | Cookware | Neither | Neither |
| B, A | Cookware | Stockpots | Neither | Neither |
| B, A | Stockpots | Cookware | B contains A | B contains A |

There are **4/4 reversal conflicts**, **4/4 relationship changes with physical
member order**, and **0/4 label changes between identical fresh repeats**.

result. This is a reproducible order-associated scope instability on one pair,
not population accuracy or observation of internal reasoning.

An independent post-call audit verifies the actual recorded provider bodies:
four request identities are each repeated twice, with eight distinct response
IDs and receipts. Repeated bodies are exact; corresponding cross-order bodies
differ only by input member order. Model, medium effort, schema, worker, wrapper

empty additional instructions and no conversation or previous-response linkage.
Producer references and final meaning strings remain exact. The recorded requests
rule out answer reuse, conversation linkage and changed prompt bytes as

Two independent reviewers inspect one draw each; their workflow familiarity is

opposite direction changes its treatment of meal purpose: containment answers

stock-making. These proposals distinguish design from actual use, but their
exclusion still depends on the disputed boundary. The unchanged strings therefore
receive incompatible treatments of that boundary. All eight overall semantic

is a demonstrated repair. Eight native agent `uncertain` reviews register that

snapshots preserve unchanged generation fields, frozen hashes, receipts and

Native `usage.json` records eight actual attempts and **$0.072474 API-equivalent

## V179 nonfood design-purpose order diagnostic

The user authorized a small diagnostic after rejecting the v109 ingredient
source edge above. This panel supplies synthetic fixed meanings directly; it
does not generate AS or Bootstrap outputs, certify original source links, replace
the existing producer-bound panels, or add validation cases to prompt examples.
Unchanged Compare-v179 and its four-field output schema are retained.

The common parent names liquid-heating vessels designed for manufacturing
chemical products intended for sale and explicitly permits open or sealed
vessels. Three sealed pressure-vessel children vary only design purpose:

| Pair | Child design purpose | Pre-call agent expectation |
|---|---|---|
| Included | Manufacturing chemical products intended for sale | Parent contains child |
| Excluded | Solely pressure-temperature measurement in basic laboratory research | Neither |
| Unrestricted | Explicitly unrestricted | Neither |

Two independent read-only preflight assessments find these scopes clear. An open
manufacturing vat establishes the reverse exclusion in every pair. A sealed
vessel designed solely for independent research measurement can escape the
manufacturing-purpose parent in the latter two pairs. The counterexamples must
preserve design intention; actual repurposing does not establish it. These are
agent-qualified controls, not user-certified gold.

The reusable [A-first source](hierarchy-compare-nonfood-purpose-a-first-v1.json)
and [B-first source](hierarchy-compare-nonfood-purpose-b-first-v1.json) each
contain the three pairs in both A/B assignments. Expected relations and review
provenance remain in evidence outside model input. The two
[A-first](hierarchy-compare-v179-nonfood-purpose-a-first-v1.spec.json) and
[B-first](hierarchy-compare-v179-nonfood-purpose-b-first-v1.spec.json)
specifications use the existing generic native adapter, shared worker,
Sol 6.1 medium/SSE and disabled explicit cache warming, matching the previous
order diagnostic's request convention. No new adapter or execution script is
needed. Create two fresh draws with native `prepare --repeat`, then
`prepare --from ... --repeat`: **3 pairs x 2 assignments x 2 member orders x
2 draws = 24 planned comparisons**. Every attempt and provider-only retry counts
against the existing shared 20,000 counter; a run's twelve-attempt ceiling allows
one temporary-provider retry per case. No unresolved request counts as success.

Before dispatch verify that corresponding rendered requests differ only in
physical input member order and that repeats have identical complete request
identity. Inspect actual recorded provider requests afterward for distinct fresh
response IDs and absence of conversation linkage or answer reuse. Evaluate
twelve reversal checks, twelve order contrasts and twelve identical-request
repeat checks, with semantic diagnostics reviewed separately from labels.

The generic schema check preserves native outputs and receipts, but does not
apply the hierarchy adapter's directional-field checks. Therefore check every
valid response read-only with existing production
`hierarchy_node_pairs.validate(..., diagnostics=True, symmetric=True)` and
register output-specific assessments through native `review`. Producer-bound
`node_pair_consistency` cannot certify directly specified synthetic endpoints;
do not fabricate Bootstrap receipts to make it do so. Read-only normalization
of native saved labels supplies the paired diagnostic counts. Native
[usage aggregation](hierarchy-compare-v179-nonfood-purpose-usage-v1.json)
retains all four experiments and actual attempt/usage accounting.

This is a bounded mechanism diagnostic with three related semantic cases,
not a population accuracy estimate. Stable controls would narrow the evidence
toward ambiguity-sensitive scope interpretation; they would not prove general
order invariance or resolve Cookware/Stockpots. A failed clear control warrants
focused instruction ablation rather than treating the ambiguous pair as gold.

### Completed nonfood purpose diagnostic

All 24 comparisons completed as fresh valid first attempts under unchanged V179.
All also pass the existing strict symmetric directional validator. Two
independent post-call reviewers each assess twelve responses and all twenty-four
directional proofs in one draw. They accept every label and directional proof,
preserving design purpose rather than inferring it from incidental actual use.
Their judgments remain agent assessments. Twenty-four output-specific native
agent accept reviews preserve those findings; `final-generation.json` and
`final-reviewed.json` in each directory retain unchanged nonreview case fields.

| Control pair | Responses matching prequalified label | Reversal conflicts | Physical-order changes | Fresh-repeat changes |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Included manufacturing purpose | 8/8 | 0/4 | 0/4 | 0/4 |
| Sole research-measurement purpose | 8/8 | 0/4 | 0/4 | 0/4 |
| Explicitly unrestricted purpose | 8/8 | 0/4 | 0/4 | 0/4 |
| Total | 24/24 | 0/12 | 0/12 | 0/12 |

These are three related fixed semantic cases tested repeatedly; the 24 responses
and overlapping contrasts are not 24 independent semantic cases. The positive
control succeeds in every assignment/order, so the pass is not produced by
returning only `neither` or `uncertain`.

An independent recorded-request audit finds 24 distinct response IDs and receipt
hashes, with twelve exact request bodies each repeated twice. Each request has
one fresh user message, no added instructions, and no conversation or previous-
response linkage. Expected relations and review evidence are absent from model
input. Prompt, schema, medium effort, worker, wrapper and request-producing code
remain identical. Input-order contrasts change only physical JSON member order;
both A/B assignments retain the exact endpoints. Every frozen source binding
resolves to this task worktree, with matching hashes. There is no answer reuse,
warmup, retry, failure, missing usage or unrun request. Ten backend prefix-cache
hits occur despite disabled explicit warming; these are not answer reuse.

Native `usage.json` reports **24 actual attempts and $0.149514 API-equivalent
catalog cost**, including reasoning in output. The shared counter reads 19,117
before dispatch and 19,141 afterward, leaving 859 at that read. It remains the
same persistent 20,000 counter; no allowance is reset or added. All native
generated artifacts stay ignored under `target/compare-v179-nonfood-purpose-order-v1`.

A read-only comparison with the original matched Stockpots draw finds the
unstable boundary is whether stock-making necessarily carries a meal-preparation
design purpose. One positive explicitly says culinary stock preparation is meal
preparation even as an intermediate ingredient; another proves food/heat without
separately proving meal purpose. The opposite-order negatives retain stock-making
design and stipulate exclusive laboratory purposes. Their exclusion remains
conditional on the unresolved stock/food/meal boundary. The clear nonfood
controls preserve all explicit purposes and sealing conditions in both directions.

The result narrows the evidence toward ambiguity-sensitive or domain-dependent
interpretation, rather than establishing a general inability to compare design
purpose. It does not distinguish lexical implications from clause attachment,
prove a causal instruction mechanism, settle either Stockpots label or fix V179.
V179 already requires unresolved judgments for materially different supported
readings, but the saved responses choose incompatible readings without closing
that issue. The next warranted test is a focused replacement of that scope-
interpretation paragraph, retaining both the original unresolved pair and these
clear positive/negative controls. No such edit or further inference is begun
as part of this completed diagnostic.

and separately inspect whether a null identifies a genuinely supported
coverage-changing boundary or merely avoids the task. Each arm has sixteen

### Completed matched V179/V184 test

All sixteen native experiments complete: **64 fresh valid first attempts**,
32 per prompt arm. Every response also passes existing strict symmetric
directional validation. The recorded requests preserve exact endpoints, schema,
worker, medium effort and code. Across arms, the only rendered-request change is
the intended scope sentence; order contrasts change only JSON member order.
Each arm has sixteen distinct request bodies, each repeated twice, with 64

request or missing usage. Seventeen backend prefix-cache reads occur; those are

|---|---:|---:|

| Control reversal conflicts | 0/12 | 0/12 |
| Control physical-order changes | 0/12 | 0/12 |
| Control repeat changes | 0/12 | 0/12 |
| Stockpots reversal conflicts | 3/4 | 4/4 |
| Stockpots physical-order changes | 3/4 | 4/4 |

V184 reproduces Cookware containment when Cookware is physically first and

and three rather than four conflicts. This small dependent panel does not
establish V184 is generally worse. It establishes that the replacement did
not repair the replicated order-associated instability. Neither arm returns
null or an `uncertain` model label; improved ambiguity handling is not observed.

An independent semantic reviewer accepts all 48 nonfood responses and both
directional proofs in every one. The included-purpose proofs retain all parent
requirements; the other pairs give separating designs and open manufacturing
vessels without confusing actual use with design intention. Separating
counterexamples establish noncontainment, not necessarily disjoint scopes.
Forty-eight native agent accept reviews register these output-specific findings.

A separate Stockpots reviewer supports all sixteen shallow-frying-pan exclusions
in the opposite direction. On a required-meal-purpose reading, it favors the
nine `neither` outputs with purpose-built research designs and finds the seven
containment proofs fail to justify necessary meal purpose. Its narrow independent
counterchallenge initially proposes a permissive reading, then withdraws it
under the instruction to preserve purpose. This is a conditional agent semantic
assessment, not a replacement for the user's reopened evaluation expectation.
The Stockpots label remains unapproved. Sixteen output-specific native agent
`uncertain` reviews retain that qualification and the reviewer's interpretation;
they do not require the model to return `uncertain` or count either disputed
label as a semantic error. Saved `final-generation.json` and `final-reviewed.json`
snapshots verify every nonreview case field remains unchanged.

Native `usage.json` reports **64 actual attempts and $0.4786384 API-equivalent
catalog cost**, reasoning included in output. The same persistent shared counter
moves from 19,141 to 19,205, leaving 795 at that read; other authorized work may
subsequently advance it. No fresh allowance, Bootstrap work or hierarchy
construction follows. All generated artifacts remain ignored in this trial's
native directories. Thirty-one relevant production-path tests pass.

V184 fails the consistency gate and is not adopted. The successful clear controls
show the edit did not cause broad uncertainty, but adding an explicit null
instruction alone has not stabilized interpretation of these familiar food
terms. There is no basis to call it a causal fix or broaden it as a promising
candidate. Before another instruction edit, the next warranted diagnostic is a
controlled input ablation that separately makes the meal-purpose boundary and
the scope of stock explicit. Use separately labeled diagnostic copies under
unchanged V179, retaining the original producer strings and evaluation panel
untouched. This can distinguish ambiguous clause scope from presumed lexical
implications; it is proposed, not prepared or executed here.

## V185 whole instruction-core qualification

### Frozen preparation and gates

The subsequent user-authorized whole-prompt audit replaces the previously
suggested input ablation as the next experiment. Experimental
[Compare-v185](Hierarchy-compare-v185.md) rewrites V179's instructional core
around one operational containment rule, with explicit binding of parent
requirements to the thing or activity supplying the information connection.
It consolidates repeated checks; it does not add a new output procedure.
The A/B subject-and-meaning contract, four-field output instructions, schema,
two synthetic examples and final input section remain unchanged. Instruction
words fall from 754 to 662; total words from 1,261 to 1,169. Native
[revision provenance](Hierarchy-compare-v185.md.revision.json) pins V179 and
records this package intervention. V185 SHA256 is
`abbc60964a81ca2eb40d8c0dc0c130edef3c711a19ad60db0d6efe82ccfa41c2`.

Independent semantic preflight retained all parent restrictions, identity,
role, intention, state and whole-alternative coverage. Five fresh synthetic
pairs were designed without seeing the candidate instructions and qualified
by two independent agent assessments before inference. They cover a kind's
repair, an activity's intended tools, particular identity versus a kind,
partially overlapping alternatives and incompatible states at one explicitly
shared reference instant. Their labels remain provisional agent expectations,
not user gold. They are outside the prompt examples and all producer-bound
regression collections. No AS or Bootstrap generation occurs.

The [A-first](hierarchy-compare-whole-core-controls-a-first-v1.json) and
[B-first](hierarchy-compare-whole-core-controls-b-first-v1.json) generic sources
combine these five pairs with the three unchanged nonfood purpose controls.
Each includes both semantic orientations; only physical JSON member order
changes between sources. Expectations, proofs and synthetic qualifications
stay outside `INPUT_JSON`. The historical Stockpots/Cookware pair retains its
exact ASv6.100 / Bootstrap-v96 endpoints through native `prepare --from` of
the saved matched-order experiments. Its correct label remains unresolved:
we evaluate interpretation, reversal and order consistency without prescribing
a positive, negative or null answer.

The first screen prepares eight native experiments under
`target/compare-v185-whole-core-v1/{v179,v185}/{stockpots,controls}`. Each
family has `a-first-01` and `b-first-01`; the Stockpots directories each have
two cases, the controls directories sixteen. This is nine semantic pairs
times two A/B assignments times two physical member orders times two prompt
arms: **72 planned fresh calls**, 36 per arm. All use intentional `--repeat`,
Sol 6.1 medium, SSE, the same worker and schema, and no explicit prefix warmup.
The shared runner's automatic queue supplies capacity; no concurrency field
is added. The existing persistent 20,000-attempt counter bounds all calls and
provider retries, with at most two lifetime attempts per case. It reads 19,205
before preparation; this is not a new allowance. Native
[usage aggregation](hierarchy-compare-v179-v185-whole-core-usage-v1.json)
separates arms and families. Request/cache/body and native receipt checks remain
required; a cache read is not an answer reuse or a conversational context.

The candidate must pass strict symmetric directional validation, all clear
semantic controls without unsupported uncertainty, and both directional proof
reviews. Consistency checks cover all reversals and physical-order contrasts,
separately for Stockpots and clear controls. A candidate failing this screen
does not receive repetitions merely to search for a favorable draw. Only a
surviving unchanged candidate warrants fresh confirmation, then broader frozen
real-data qualification before adoption. Stable labels alone do not establish
semantic correctness. This package comparison can establish a candidate
difference, not which removed sentence caused it; any successful package would
need targeted add-back diagnostics before such a causal claim.

Official reasoning guidance supports clear, direct instructions and concise
auditable diagnostic conclusions instead of requested reasoning traces;
evaluation guidance identifies position bias and recommends clear rubrics and
human alignment. Neither endorses this particular containment convention or
guarantees the rewrite will work. The chosen Sol-medium workload must supply
that evidence. Sources: [reasoning guidance](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/reasoning-best-practices#how-to-prompt-reasoning-models-effectively)
and [evaluation guidance](https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/evaluation-best-practices#llm-as-a-judge-and-model-graders).

### Completed first screen

All eight native experiments finish with **72 fresh valid first attempts**,
36 per arm. Existing production `hierarchy_node_pairs.validate` accepts every
response under `diagnostics=True, symmetric=True`. No provider retry, failure,
unrun request, exact answer reuse, explicit warmup or missing usage occurs.
The same persistent counter moves from 19,205 to 19,277. Native `usage.json`
reports **$0.4713536 API-equivalent catalog cost**, including reasoning in
output; this is not subscription billing. Twenty-four backend cache reads
are distinct from answer reuse or conversational context.

| First-screen measure | Fresh V179 | Experimental V185 |
|---|---:|---:|
| Clear-control expected labels | 32/32 | 32/32 |
| Clear-control reversal conflicts | 0/16 | 0/16 |
| Clear-control physical-order changes | 0/16 | 0/16 |
| Stockpots reversal conflicts | 1/2 | 2/2 |
| Stockpots physical-order changes | 1/2 | 2/2 |

V185 again concludes Cookware contains Stockpots when Cookware is physically
first, and `neither` when it is second. Its first-position positive proofs
infer meal preparation from stock preparation; its second-position negative
proofs admit an experimental stock-preparation design without that intention.
The two unchanged definitions do not justify changing interpretation according
to position. Fresh V179 has three `neither` results and one containment result,
so it also fails consistency. Four responses per arm on one disputed pair
cannot establish general worsening or a reliable error rate. The expected
Stockpots relation remains unresolved; these counts identify inconsistent
interpretation rather than certifying which semantic answer must change.

The candidate fails the declared consistency gate. No second draw, broader
panel or adoption follows from this screen. The package preserves clear
label behavior but has not repaired the original failure. It does not prove
that clarity, brevity, question generation or any removed individual sentence
causes the residual error. Original endpoints and all frozen producer panels
remain untouched.

Seventy-three existing production-path node-pair tests pass. An initial test
invocation also named a nonexistent additional module; the corrected command
runs the actual supported node-pair suite alone. The read-only output audit
uses the existing validator's returned parsed result and exceptions, not an
invented `valid` flag. No production code or test APIs are changed.

Independent semantic reviews accept all **64 clear-control responses**, including
all 128 directional questions, answers and proofs. Repair and tool information
remain covered without requiring physical instance membership or performed
action; identity counterexamples do not assign unknown Lumen properties;
alternative and state exclusions retain the whole supplied requirements.
Three identity responses use battery-capacity wording with a justification
identifying the distinct qualifying battery-powered lamp. Review accepts the
combined evidence while recording this minor standalone wording qualification.
The reviewers projected input and output, without reading candidate instructions
or expected-label fields. Directory names still exposed version identifiers,
so this is not a fully arm-blinded study. After completing its V185 judgments,
one reviewer saw the already-completed H5 design summary through an agent-status
call; it was not available before those judgments. The delegated V179 review
remained independently blinded to that summary.

A separate Stockpots review distinguishes five conditional `neither` arguments
from three conditional containment arguments. If Cookware universally requires
meal-preparation design and Stockpots does not, the experimental designs supply
valid separating cases. The three positives instead infer meal intention from
stock or food preparation without independently establishing that implication.
If the meal clause only illustrates unrestricted food-preparation design, the
experimental cases do not escape the parent. These conditional assessments
do not resolve the user's original label. V185's `b-first-01`,
`isolated-035-reverse` also leaves its shallow-pan counterexample's meal-design
admission under-established on the restrictive reading; frying eggs is not an
explicit intention proof in this response. We do not transfer favorable
interpretations from other outputs to repair that evidence.

Native output-specific agent reviews accept the 64 controls and mark the eight
original Stockpots cases uncertain with these qualifications. Their original
model labels are preserved. This review verdict does not require the model to
return `uncertain`; it records unresolved semantic gold. `final-generation.json`
and native `final-reviewed.json` preserve original outputs and receipts;
reviews do not alter any nonreview case field. No homemade evaluation artifacts
or producer receipts are substituted for the native record.

The next warranted diagnostic is now a controlled boundary test under an
unchanged prompt: separately distinguish universal meal-design intention from
an illustrative meal application, then explicit meal intention from food
identity in Stockpots. Retain the original producer strings and disputed gold
untouched. Test one boundary at a time in both assignments and member orders.
That can determine whether the residual comparison failure survives genuinely
closed scopes before another instruction rewrite. It is a recommendation,
not prepared or run here. No Bootstrap coordination, hierarchy generation or
additional allowance is implied.

The independent native identity audit verifies all 72 actual request bodies and
receipt-to-result links. Model, effort, endpoint, worker, SSE settings, ordered
schema and frozen executable code match across arms; 905 recorded source-file
hashes remain unchanged. The only arm-specific body change is the instruction
prefix before `# Output`. Order variants change only top-level JSON member
order; all 36 within-arm reversal comparisons exchange the exact endpoints.
All eight Stockpots cases retain original baseline inputs and evidence, both
Bootstrap-v96 producer meanings/receipts, and the original ASv6.100 post/tree
provenance. Seventy-two production receipt validations, request/output hashes
and unique response IDs pass. Each actual body contains exactly one user
prompt, empty instructions, `store:false` and no conversation linkage. Native

explains away the observed comparison inconsistency.

## Fixed-V179 meal-design boundary diagnostic

### Frozen design

After the failed V185 screen, the user authorizes testing explicit meal-design
intention versus food identity under **unchanged Compare-v179**. No Compare
prompt, Bootstrap prompt, producer meaning or hierarchy is changed. The exact
original Cookware/Stockpots case remains a descriptive control, inherited
through native `prepare --from`; its correct label remains unresolved.

Four directly supplied diagnostic copies close the disputed boundaries. Both
parent copies keep the same food-holding/heat-cooking design and qualifying
cocotte/frying-pan illustrations. Both expressly count edible culinary stock
as food regardless of intended consumption. Their final clause differs only
by `must` versus `need not` require meal preparation as a design purpose.
Both child copies explicitly retain food, holding, heat cooking and stock
preparation, the same food-identity sentence and a laboratory-design purpose.
Their final clause differs only by `also` versus `not` include meal preparation
as a design purpose. A dual laboratory-and-meal design is coherent; actual use
or consumption is not substituted for design intention. Hypothetical permitted
designs supply counterexamples without asserting actual instances exist.

Two independent read-only preflight assessments qualify the exact closed
matrix: meal-included Stockpots fit either parent; meal-excluded Stockpots fit
only the unrestricted parent. Direct parent-copy comparison requires the
unrestricted copy to contain the required copy; direct child-copy comparison
is neither. The classifications are provisional agent semantic controls,
not human gold or certified Bootstrap outputs. Physical exclusion between
designs is not itself the definition of information-topic containment: both
directional proofs must also exclude permitted information connections.

The full 3-by-3 cross of original/required/unrestricted parents and
original/meal-included/meal-excluded children has nine conditions. Add the
two direct same-subject contrasts: eleven semantic pairs total. Six fully
synthetic conditions are closed semantic anchors. The original and four
one-original bridges are descriptive with null expected labels; they must not
select the original interpretation by majority vote. Even the apparently
positive original-parent/meal-included-child bridge is kept outside the
closed-anchor gate. Direct same-subject failure would establish failure to
honor the supplied definitions, not prove that names caused it.

The [A-first](hierarchy-compare-meal-design-boundary-a-first-v1.json) and
[B-first](hierarchy-compare-meal-design-boundary-b-first-v1.json) generic sources
carry twenty diagnostic cases each: ten conditions in both A/B mappings.
The exact original two cases in each order are inherited separately. All
copies use the original native JSON serialization; native preparation must
assemble identical instructions, schema and request convention across
adapters, apart from supplied endpoint descriptions and controlled order.
Expectations, source qualifications and original provenance remain outside
model input. No generated parent meaning, post, local tree or old label enters
the requests.

The first block prepares four native directories under
`target/compare-v179-meal-design-boundary-v1/{original,diagnostic}`, with
`a-first-01` and `b-first-01`: **44 planned fresh calls**. All use intentional
`--repeat`, Sol 6.1 medium/SSE, the same worker and four-field schema, unchanged
V179/examples, no explicit warming and the shared automatic queue. The existing
persistent 20,000-attempt counter reads 19,277 before preparation; provider
retries/failures count, with at most two lifetime attempts per case. Native
[first-block usage](hierarchy-compare-v179-meal-design-boundary-first-screen-usage-v1.json)
and [conditional aggregate](hierarchy-compare-v179-meal-design-boundary-usage-v1.json)
preserve separate original/diagnostic accounting.

Only if all six closed anchors pass labels, directional proof review, strict
validation, reversal and member-order checks does an unchanged second fresh
block follow: another 44 calls, **88 total absent retries**. The disputed
original/bridge labels are evaluated descriptively rather than used as gold
gates. Native request/receipt and independent-context checks remain required.
The result can distinguish following explicit design clauses from unstable
comparison of closed scopes. It cannot certify the original relation or name
the original reversal's causal word: moving to a diagnostic copy changes
several lexical facts. Within the closed matrix, food identity and laboratory
purpose are fixed while meal-design intention is controlled.

### Completed two-block diagnostic

The first block passes its gate: all 24 closed-anchor responses match their
provisional expectations, and independent review supports both proofs and
questions in all 24. Original/bridge outcomes remain descriptive. The
unchanged second block is prepared natively with `--from ...-01 --repeat`;
prompt, cases, runner, schema and each adapter's code bindings are identical
between draws. Its 24 closed-anchor responses also match and pass fresh
independent review. Both blocks therefore complete with **88 valid responses**,
including **48/48 supported closed-anchor responses and 96 directional proofs**.

| Response family | Responses | Reversal conflicts | Member-order changes | Repeat changes |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Six closed semantic anchors | 48 | 0/24 | 0/24 | 0/24 |
| Four one-original bridges | 32 | 0/16 | 0/16 | 0/16 |
| Exact original Cookware/Stockpots pair | 8 | 3/4 | 3/4 | 1/4 |

Every diagnostic-copy condition gives the same normalized label in all eight
presentations/draws. Required Cookware rejects meal-excluded Stockpots while
unrestricted Cookware contains them; both parents contain meal-included
Stockpots. Unrestricted Cookware contains the required variant, and the two
opposite Stockpots purpose variants are neither despite identical subjects.
This is evidence that V179 can follow the explicit meal-design distinction
while retaining food identity and laboratory purpose. It is not broad Compare
qualification or certification of these manually supplied scopes as producer
definitions.

All four bridges are stable: original Cookware contains meal-included
Stockpots; original Cookware versus meal-excluded Stockpots is neither;
required Cookware versus original Stockpots is neither; unrestricted Cookware
contains original Stockpots. The labels alone do not adjudicate an original
meaning. The latter two proofs still rely on an interpretation of the
original Stockpots wording. Likewise, rejection beneath original Cookware
assumes its meal clause imposes a universal design requirement. Closing either
endpoint stabilizes these observations, but several lexical facts change in
moving to a copy, so this does not isolate the original causal word.

The untouched original has five `neither` and three Cookware-containment
responses. Its positive proofs promote stock preparation to meal preparation;
negative proofs admit exclusively experimental food-stock designs without
meal intention. Both meanings and the instructions remain identical between
these readings. No model nulls are emitted. Original semantic gold remains
unresolved; the observed contradiction is not repaired by stable copies,
majority vote or selecting whichever original label resembles an anchor.

Native aggregate usage records **91 actual attempts**, including three
recovered provider failures, zero answer reuse or warming and no terminal
failed/unrun cases. The shared counter moves **19,277 to 19,368**, leaving 632
at this read. All three failed body streams are classified by the production
runner as temporary HTTP/2 internal stream errors after HTTP 200, with socket
termination and no completed answer; the same cases succeed on attempt two.
Each failed attempt remains charged to the counter and has unknown usage.
The **$0.610562 API-equivalent cost is a known subtotal**, not an exact total
or subscription charge. Native usage preserves the three unknowns rather
than pricing them at zero. Provider retries remain within two lifetime
attempts per case; no service investigation, paid-resource change or external
message is initiated.

Existing strict symmetric directional validation accepts all 88 responses.
The request-generating code, schema, V179 instructions/examples and native
JSON serialization match between real controls and generic copies. An initial
overstrict plan-code assertion detects only the extra
`hierarchy_node_pair_consistency.py` bound by the original adapter; that is a
post-generation evaluator, absent from the generic adapter, not a request
difference. All shared code hashes match. Current source meanings remain
unchanged, and original Bootstrap-v96 / ASv6.100 receipts are not attached to
diagnostic copies as if those copies had been generated.

The final independent provenance audit validates all 91 dispatched bodies,
88 successful receipts and generation-snapshot bindings. Each body carries
one exact user prompt, empty instructions, `store:false` and no conversation
linkage. Completed response IDs are unique; all 904 bound source hashes match.
All 44 member-order contrasts change only JSON member order and all 44
reversal comparisons exchange the exact endpoints. Recovered attempts repeat
identical bodies. Native original producer receipts and ASv6.100 inputs remain
intact. Backend cache reads do not create conversation linkage.

Separate original/bridge proof review checks all 40 responses across both
draws. The 32 bridges retain the explicit food and design clauses consistently.
Their qualifications remain conditional where an original endpoint supplies
the interpretation: eight unrestricted-parent/original-child positives rely
on the culinary-food reading of original stock. Among the eight exact
original outputs, five negative proofs conditionally separate experimental
designs; three positives have meal-purpose proof gaps under the universal
meal-design interpretation. Two positive original outputs also under-establish
their independent frying-pan witness's meal intention. These assessments do
not yield an accuracy percentage against original gold.

Native reviews accept the 48 closed-anchor responses and preserve uncertainty
for all 40 original/bridge responses. Original model relations remain unchanged.
Native `final-generation.json` and `final-reviewed.json` verify all nonreview
fields and receipts are preserved. No homemade comparison or review artifacts
replace the supported workflow.

The practical finding is narrower than a Compare fix: these closed definitions
are handled consistently under unchanged V179, while the original combination
retains unstable interpretation. Another instruction reminder is not justified
by this evidence. The next useful qualification would use separately frozen,
current producer meanings whose relevant boundaries have been independently
settled, then test reversals and complete chains without forcing old labels.
This recommendation is not prepared or run here. The present source copies
must not be silently promoted to producer definitions or human gold.

The metal-handles probe separately contrasts required handholds with grips:

The fifty-case first draw uses 51 attempts including one warmup, with no failures,

The [source](hierarchy-compare-hr05-fixed-scopes-source-v1.json) selects six exact

Both orientations below are normalized to the original source's A/B assignment;

| Original pair | V179 forward / reversed | V186 forward / reversed |
|---|---|---|
| Food / Bread | A contains B / A contains B | A contains B / A contains B |
| Equipment / Lunch boxes | neither / A contains B | neither / neither |
| Commercial entities / Buster + Punch | A contains B / A contains B | uncertain / uncertain |

The strongest new fixed-scope witness is V186's intention/suitability pair.
Its forward response admits a safe edible, nutritionally inert preparation
mistakenly intended to nourish. The reverse asserts that the identical intended
scope necessarily establishes suitability as nourishment. These incompatible
necessity claims establish a Compare consistency failure independently of which
complete label is approved. The reverse proof does not establish the missing
implication. Preserve the exact pair as a diagnostic; do not choose a semantic
target merely to make the reversal agree.

V179's Lunch boxes forward similarly admits an edible container while its
reverse asserts all qualifying containers are non-food. V186's two negatives
are consistent, but not a certified correction: the phrase "tools, apparatus,
and other non-food physical articles designed..." permits competing readings
of which alternatives bear those restrictions. An edible tool/apparatus must
also be excluded before the counterexample proves noncontainment. The parent
grammar and complete semantic label remain qualified.

All four Bread positives infer nourishment intention from the ordinary food
kind without separately closing purpose versus nutritional capability. The
restaurant responses likewise choose different ordinary-kind commerce readings;
charitable provision and the word "customers" need qualification. Matching
labels do not certify either family's proof. V179's brand positive relies on
actual commercial offerings not separately stated in the supplied named-brand
definition; V186 follows the unknown-property reading in this draw. That
behavioral change certifies neither the brand's semantic label nor a general
reliability improvement.

Two independent semantic reviews agree on the concrete contradictions and
preserve these qualifications. The native checker scopes saved endpoints to
their original comparison receipt and original role; it intentionally does not
join coincident wording from separate cases into producer-certified chains.
No cross-source chain result or Bootstrap/hierarchy repair is claimed.
**V186 remains experimental and is not adopted.** V179 remains the development
baseline, with its own demonstrated conflicts retained.

`target/compare-hr05-fixed-scopes-v1/{v179,v186}-native.json`; corresponding

## Compare-v187: necessary coverage conditions

The user authorizes a rapid edit/test/review loop with independent subagents.
[Experimental V187](Hierarchy-compare-v187.md) is a single Full coverage
paragraph replacement from the V179 development baseline; it does not carry
forward V186's separately unqualified particular-entity revision. Each applicable
parent condition needs a child condition or necessary implication. Intention
alone does not establish actual capability, suitability or success; sharing a
kind name alone cannot supply additional restrictions. The A/B input, four
output fields, schema and existing examples remain byte-identical.

The [six synthetic controls](hierarchy-compare-intention-capability-controls-v1.json)
use independently specified device scopes: intention I, current capability C,
and their intersection IC. I/C is provisionally neither; I/IC and C/IC have
strict containment. Both orientations are included. Counterexamples must preserve
current state and exclusive purpose and exclude permitted information connections;
merely switching off a device or omitting an intention is insufficient. These
are reviewed agent controls, not AS/Bootstrap outputs, human gold, or prompt
examples. No original source post or producer receipt is fabricated.

The first gate comprises the unchanged twelve native HR05 cases inherited
from the saved V179 run and six new generic controls: **eighteen candidate
comparisons**, plus two eligible automatic warmups. The saved V179 HR05 baseline
is reused; it is not rerun. No matched synthetic V179 draw is claimed.
Native preparations and generation remain in the task worktree under
`target/compare-v187-intention-capability-v1/{hr05,controls}`. Both use the shared
runner, Sol 6.1 medium/SSE, automatic caching and the existing persistent 20,000
attempt counter. Stage caps reserve provider retries; they are not new allowances.

Predeclared passing criteria are valid complete generation; six supported control
labels and directional proofs; the two exact user-approved MrBeast positives;
no HR05 reversal conflict; and no demonstrated unsupported necessity claim,
dropped parent alternative, or invented particular-entity fact. Real probes keep
null semantic expectations. Warranted uncertainty remains qualified rather than
being counted as a correction, and clear controls cannot be cleared by returning
uncertain/neither indiscriminately. Generic controls have manual independent
semantic/reversal review and the existing strict production comparison validator;
the native receipt-bound checker covers HR05, without invented producer identities
for synthetic cases.

A failing candidate receives no broader confirmation. Revise one demonstrated
mechanism in a new version and repeat the small gate. A promising candidate needs
an unchanged fresh confirmation and broader frozen-input regressions before an
adoption recommendation. Keep source cohorts separate, preserve original
meanings/receipts and count all dispatched attempts including warmups and failures.
The [native first-gate usage source](hierarchy-compare-v187-intention-capability-gate-usage-v1.json)
keeps HR05 and synthetic groups separate. No Bootstrap edit/inference, hierarchy
construction, UI refresh or outgoing owner message is part of this work.

### V187 first-gate failure and V188 local contrast

V187 completes all eighteen valid responses. All six synthetic labels and both
MrBeast positives survive. The HR05 intention/suitability and Equipment labels
are reciprocal in this draw, but Food/Bread has a new reversal conflict:
the forward treats the child word "food" as necessarily supplying nourishing
intention, while the reverse admits ceremonial bread explicitly lacking it.
This is incompatible necessity selection for an unchanged meaning, not proof
of which complete label is correct. V187 fails and receives no repeat or broad
qualification. The two camera control witnesses retain a precision qualification
about actual detection versus mere image capture; no autonomous-classification
requirement is added. Equipment's grammar and intention-to-design claim remain
qualified, and the brand proofs still import actual product offerings beyond
the fixed string. No failed probe is made semantic gold.

Two independent reviews and native receipt/request audit validate the inputs,
twenty distinct responses including warmups, strict output checks and unchanged
baseline bindings. Native usage is twenty attempts, zero reuse/retries/failures/
missing usage, twelve comparison cache hits and $0.1394824 API-equivalent cost.
The shared counter subsequently reads 19,931/20,000; other agents' admissions
are included there, not in this gate's twenty calls.

[Experimental V188](Hierarchy-compare-v188.md) preserves V187's coverage paragraph
and changes only the kind-interpretation sentence for the local contrast. It
requires interpreting a term within its own meaning, without borrowing the other
node's definition or treating customary function/capability as a required
intention. This tests two related explanations for the Bread failure; it does
not prove either word caused it or force ceremonial-Bread admission. The native
full revision remains against V179, with the retained V187 change explicitly
recorded. Schema, A/B input and examples remain unchanged. The same eighteen-case
gate and passing criteria apply under the remaining shared allowance; no new
cap or reset is introduced.

### V188 gate: reciprocal labels, remaining particular-fact proof gap

The unchanged eighteen-case gate completes with eighteen valid responses:
all six provisional synthetic labels and both exact user-approved MrBeast
positives survive, and the native HR05 checker finds zero reversal conflicts
among six complete pairs. Food/Bread and intention/suitability now use reciprocal
intention-based readings. This is one draw, not proof of causal wording,
semantic gold for the real probes, or an adoption recommendation.

Two independent reviewers accept the six controls and MrBeast proofs. Both
identify the remaining brand proof issue: coverage is established by adding
particular lighting, hardware or commercial design-goods offerings absent from
the fixed "London design brand" meaning. These might be true external facts,
but are contingent rather than necessary implications of that fixed string.
The predeclared proof-quality gate is therefore not cleared. Equipment's
coordinated non-food modifier and the restaurant commerce reading remain
qualifications, not newly forced labels. Native agent reviews preserve the
ten real-probe uncertainties and eight accepted control/preservation cases.

Native exports are `target/compare-v188-intention-capability-v1/{hr05,controls}-native.json`;
the native aggregate `usage.json` records twenty actual attempts including two
